Furthermore, we introduce three prompt contrastive learning strategies, which enable effective learning of prompts corresponding to unseen modality combinations, thereby significantly strengthening the model's generalization capabilities in diverse testing scenarios. Extensive experiments on three widely used datasets demonstrate that $\textsf{CMPL}$ achieves more than a 5% improvement in accuracy compared to state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [110] [A three-dimensional typology of agency for advanced AI systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20041)
*Willem Fourie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对前沿AI系统提出了一种三维主体性（agency）类型学框架，涵盖主体性的本质（道德或法律）、模式（个体或集体）以及位置（人类或非人类），组合产生八种可能的主体性实例，并区分为传统型、争议型和易引发争议型。该框架将法律主体性与道德主体性区分开来，为在不预设前沿AI是道德主体的前提下，讨论个体的、法律的、非人类的主体性创造了概念空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于先进AI系统主体性的研究主要将主体性视为规范性概念，并聚焦于特别"具有主体性"的AI系统。虽然近期工作关注不同主体性系统的特征，但仍缺乏一个框架来回答先进AI系统实例化了何种类型的主体性，尤其是在考虑非道德形式的主体性时。

Method: 基于哲学、伦理学、法学理论和社会学中既有的理论立场，开发了一个由三个维度组成的前沿AI系统主体性类型学：主体性的本质（道德或法律）、模式（个体或集体）和位置（人类或非人类）。将三个维度组合产生八种可能的主体性实例，并将其分类为传统型（conventional）、争议型（contested）或易引发争议型（controversial）。

Result: 该类型学将法律主体性与道德主体性分离，从而为考虑个体的、法律的、非人类的主体性创造了概念空间，而不必预设先进AI系统是道德主体。作者论证了这一区分在工具性目标追求使AI行为向特定人类行为者的归因复杂化时越来越具有相关性。

Conclusion: 通过区分法律与道德主体性并提供系统的分类框架，本文为分析前沿AI系统所实例化的主体性类型提供了概念工具，特别适用于处理AI在工具性目标追求中行为归因日益复杂的问题。

Abstract: Research on the agency of advanced artificial intelligence (AI) systems focuses on agency as a normative concept and on the agency of particularly agentic AI systems. While recent work also focuses on the different profiles of agentic systems, no framework exists to address the question of the type of agency instantiated by advanced AI systems, particularly when considering non-moral forms of agency. Based on established theoretical positions in philosophy, ethics, legal theory and sociology, we develop a typology of agency for frontier AI systems consisting of three dimensions: the nature of agency (moral or legal), its mode (individual or collective) and its locus (human or non-human). Combining these dimensions produces eight possible instantiations of agency, which we classify as conventional, contested or controversial. The typology separates legal from moral agency and thereby creates conceptual space for considering individual, legal, non-human agency without presupposing that advanced AI systems are moral agents. We argue that this distinction is increasingly relevant where instrumental goal pursuit complicates the attribution of AI actions to particular human actors.

</details>


### [111] [On the Applicability of Safety Nets: A Safety-By-Design Solution for Certifying Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20053)
*Johann Maximilian Christensen,Thomas Stefani,Elena Hoemann,Frank Köster,Sven Hallerbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统分析了Safety Nets中神经网络与查找表之间的规模权衡，通过对比多种神经网络架构，发现3-5个隐藏层、每层50-100个节点并结合one-hot编码可实现最佳平衡。该配置下神经网络至少准确表示97%的数据，紧凑查找表处理其余误差，系统整体缩小近三个数量级，满足EASA认证要求并保证100%正确的运行时行为。同时提供了首个开源的Safety Nets实现（针对HCAS和VCAS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能在航空安全关键系统中的集成面临认证与部署的重大挑战。虽然EASA要求采用Safety-by-Design方法（通过Safety Nets实现），且Safety Nets已被研究，但尚未有对其性能特征和系统设计权衡进行全面分析的研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，进行首次系统性分析。

Method: 系统性地比较具有不同架构的神经网络，评估神经网络压缩与查找表组合的Safety Nets方案。通过探索网络层数、节点数、编码方式等设计参数，识别能够最小化整体存储与内存需求、同时维持认证合规性的最优设计。

Result: 具有3至5个隐藏层、每层约50至100个节点的架构，结合one-hot编码能实现最佳平衡。这些配置下神经网络至少准确表示97%的数据，紧凑查找表处理剩余错误。Safety Nets将系统大小减少近三个数量级，可在当前航空硬件内存预算内运行，并保证整个离散化输入空间的100%正确输出。

Conclusion: Safety Nets被确立为航空安全关键应用中可行的Safety-by-Design解决方案，提供了通向可认证AI系统的实用路径。首个开源实现（HCAS和VCAS）保证了结果的可复现性，为未来安全关键AI系统的认证与部署奠定了基础。

Abstract: The integration of Artificial Intelligence (AI) in safety-critical aviation systems presents significant challenges for certification and deployment. Aviation, often regarded as the safest form of transportation, relies on numerous safety-critical systems. For future safety-critical AI-based systems, EASA requires a Safety-by-Design approach, which can be achieved by using Safety Nets that combine neural network compression with lookup tables to ensure 100 % correct runtime behavior across the discretized operational design domain. Although Safety Nets have been studied, no comprehensive study of their performance characteristics and system design trade-offs has been conducted. This work presents the first systematic analysis of the trade-off between neural network and lookup table size in Safety Nets. By systematically comparing neural networks with diverse architectures, this study identifies optimal design parameters that minimize overall storage and memory requirements while maintaining certification compliance. Results demonstrate that architectures with 3 to 5 hidden layers, each with approximately 50 to 100 nodes, combined with one-hot encoding, achieve the best balance. In these configurations, neural networks accurately represent at least 97 % of the data, while compact lookup tables handle the remaining errors. The resulting Safety Nets reduce the system size by almost three orders of magnitude, fitting within the memory budget of current avionics hardware while guaranteeing 100 % correct outputs across the entire discretized input space, as required by EASA guidelines. This work provides the first-ever open-source implementation of Safety Nets for HCAS and VCAS with replicable results, demonstrating a practical pathway toward certifiable AI-based systems in aviation and establishing Safety Nets as a viable Safety-by-Design solution for safety-critical applications.

</details>


### [112] [What You Can't See Is What You Learn: Restricted Evidence Visibility Favors Compositional Generalization in Shared-Genome Language-Model Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20054)
*Narcis Marincat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在多模块系统中限制证据可见性（而非让每个模块看到完整输入）是否会影响梯度训练所发现的解决方案。实验基于共享冻结预训练语言模型和低秩适配器的"四元胞社会"，通过固定中继仅以两个模型宽度的连续向量通信。在10个匹配的受限/全局可见对照对（仅注意力掩码不同）中，受限社会在9/10对中表现优于全局可见样本20分以上，中位配对优势为0.7648和0.6050。完全切断通信使受限社会性能降至随机水平；在训练中从未出现的组合函数上仍有优势。审计显示受限社会的同值数据包移植可保持行为（0.94-1.00），破坏性干预导致崩溃，反事实数据包将输出导向数学预测答案。尽管受限可见性大幅提高泛化中继概率并偏好可复用的值索引接口，但该预注册实验正式失败——受限臂中位深度三准确率为0.6988，低于0.70门槛；合格队列也仅0/10完全通过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模块系统通常将完整输入暴露给每个模块。作者希望检验一种不同的设计假设：限制证据可见性是否会改变梯度训练发现解的方式，从而影响系统组合能力与泛化性能。

Method: 构建"四元胞社会"系统：四个单元共享一个冻结的预训练语言模型和低秩适配器，通过两个模型宽度的连续向量在固定中继中传递信息。在新密封的自然语言函数组合任务上，训练10对匹配的受限/全局可见对照，这些对照对共享初始化字节、训练顺序、词元布局、参数和计算，唯一差异是注意力掩码。

Result: 受限社会在9/10配对中在两个深度上均优于全局可见孪生至少20分，中位配对优势为0.7648和0.6050。切断通信会使受限社会降至随机水平。深度三优势在组合函数从未出现在训练中的程序上仍为0.558。跨六个受限社会的审计表明：同值数据包移植保持行为（0.94-1.00），破坏性干预导致崩溃，反事实数据包将输出导向数学预测答案。但预注册实验正式失败：受限臂中位深度三准确率0.6988低于0.70门槛；合格队列仅0/10完全通过——虽然一个模型通过所有任务性能门槛，但全部10个在普通语言保留上失败。

Conclusion: 受限可见性并非组合能力的必要条件，但在此协议下它显著提高了泛化中继出现的概率，并促使模型发展出可复用、值索引化的接口。然而系统未能通过完整的预注册门槛，且由于普通语言保留失败，该系统只能限于显式任务门控的使用场景。

Abstract: Multi-module systems often expose every module to the full input. We test whether restricting evidence visibility changes which solutions gradient-based training discovers. Four-cell societies share one frozen pretrained language model and one low-rank adapter, communicating only through two model-width continuous vectors in a fixed relay. On a prospectively sealed natural-language function-composition task, we train ten matched restricted/global pairs sharing initialization bytes, training order, token layout, parameters, and computation; only the attention mask differs. Restricted societies outperform their globally visible twins by at least 20 points at both depths in 9 of 10 pairs, with median paired advantages of 0.7648 and 0.6050. Cutting communication reduces every restricted society to chance, and the depth-three advantage remains 0.558 on programs whose composite function never appeared in training. Across six audited restricted societies, same-value packet transplants preserve behavior at 0.94-1.00 across all tested interfaces; destructive interventions collapse performance; and counterfactual packets redirect outputs toward the mathematically predicted answer. The sole high-performing global model also requires communication, but its same-value packets are not interchangeable across episodes. Restricted visibility is thus not necessary for composition; under this protocol it substantially increases the probability of a generalizing relay and favors a reusable, value-indexed interface. The complete preregistered battery nevertheless formally fails because restricted-arm median depth-three accuracy is 0.6988, below the 0.70 floor. An earlier qualification cohort likewise yielded 0/10 complete passes: one model met every task-performance gate, but all ten failed ordinary-language preservation, confining the system to explicitly task-gated use.

</details>


### [113] [DARS: Dual-Level Credit Assignment RL with Structured Reasoning for Instruction-Based Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20161)
*Haoxiang Cao,Jiajiong Cao,Xuanpu Zhang,Changqian Yu,Chaoqun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DARS是一个面向指令式图像编辑的双层级（跨模块+模块内）信用分配强化学习框架。跨模块层面通过多计划多渲染rollout估计奖励变异性实现软模块路由，并利用rollout均值奖励构建自适应课程；规划器内部通过四字段结构化推理输出实现前缀门控奖励与token级优势重加权。在五个基准上超越同配置的Joint RL基线，推理密集型编辑收益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在planner-renderer两阶段图像编辑管线中，仅用最终图像奖励训练效率低下：差的编辑结果无法判断优化应侧重planner还是renderer，且即使问题出在planner侧，也难在自由形式的推理轨迹中定位具体错误位置。现有方法缺乏跨模块与模块内的双层级信用分配机制。

Method: 1) 跨模块：多计划多渲染rollout估计计划间与计划内奖励变异性，实现软模块路由（soft module routing）；利用rollout均值奖励作为难度估计构建自适应课程（adaptive curriculum）。2) 模块内（planner）：设计四字段结构化推理输出，实现前缀门控奖励（prefix-gated reward）与token级优势重加权（token-level advantage reweighting），将结果级反馈转化为局部监督信号。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上，DARS在保持相同backbone、数据、奖励模型和rollout预算的条件下，全面超越Joint RL基线；在推理密集型编辑任务上取得最大提升。

Conclusion: 通过双层级信用分配，即跨模块的软路由与课程学习、以及规划器内部的结构化奖励归因，能够更高效地将稀疏的结果级奖励转化为可定位的模块级和token级监督，从而显著提升指令式图像编辑性能，尤其适用于推理密集场景。

Abstract: Instruction-based image editing uses a planner-renderer pipeline: a vision-language model (VLM) first converts the instruction into an edit plan, and a diffusion model then executes that plan. Training such systems with only final-image rewards is inefficient because a poor edit does not reveal whether additional optimization should place more emphasis on the planner or the renderer, and even planner-dominant cases remain difficult to localize within a free-form reasoning trace. We present DARS, a reinforcement learning framework for dual-level credit assignment in this two-stage setting. Across modules, multi-plan multi-render rollouts estimate between-plan and within-plan reward variability for soft module routing, while rollout mean rewards provide hardness estimates for an adaptive curriculum. Within the planner, a four-field structured reasoning output enables a prefix-gated reward and token-level advantage reweighting, turning outcome-level feedback into localized supervision. Experiments on five benchmarks show that DARS outperforms a Joint~RL baseline with the same backbone, data, reward model, and rollout budget, with the largest gains on reasoning-intensive edits.

</details>


### [114] [The Third Restructuring of Software Form: From the Three-Tier Architecture to Storage, Models, and Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20201)
*Wei Lin,Tao Zhou,Zhaofei Xie,Changgui Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出软件形态经历了从软件1.0（指令驱动行为）、软件2.0（数据驱动行为，即机器学习）到软件3.0（上下文与推理驱动行为）的第三次范式转移。软件3.0的最终形态由三个核心要素构成：广义数据库（所有持久状态和记忆的统一抽象）、大型模型（执行推理和生成的智能核心）以及智能体（连接前两者的执行循环）。论文形式化了这一收敛论点，提出最小参考架构，并用真实原型和在线模型提供证据支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到软件工程的历史演变——从指令驱动的软件1.0到数据驱动的软件2.0，认为当前正进入由上下文与推理驱动行为的第三阶段（软件3.0）。传统三层架构中，UI层将被模型按需生成界面的能力吸收，业务逻辑层将按"可表达性×关键性"重新划分（分为模型推理与存储约束，剩余确定性逻辑保留为工具），唯独数据层升华为唯一的持久基础设施。作者希望系统性地论证这一收敛论断，并明确其成立条件与失败边界。

Method: 论文采用理论与实践结合的方法：(1) 形式化定义软件3.0的收敛论点与最小参考架构；(2) 将传统三层架构的UI层、业务逻辑层和数据层分别映射到新范式下的模型生成、模型推理+存储约束/工具、以及持久基础设施；(3) 通过真实原型和在线模型的实证证据验证论点；(4) 系统分析论点的成立条件（可表达、可验证、外部有状态、工具完备的任务域）与失败边界（确定性、成本、安全性、可验证性四个维度）。

Result: 论文的核心成果是：(1) 提出了软件3.0的收敛论题及其三个核心组成要素（广义数据库、大模型、智能体）；(2) 给出了实现该收敛的最小参考架构；(3) 通过原型和在线模型提供了实证证据；(4) 明确了论点的适用边界——它成立的条件是可表达、可验证、外部有状态且工具完备的任务域，而在确定性、成本、安全和可验证性等方面存在约束边界。

Conclusion: 作者认为软件3.0的论题在可表达、可验证、外部有状态且工具完备的任务领域内成立，这一范式转移将重塑开发者角色、数据库行业以及软件工程学科本身。同时，论文诚实地指出了确定性、成本、安全性和可验证性这四个维度构成了论点的适用边界，在超出这些边界时传统软件工程范式仍然必要。

Abstract: Software form has undergone two paradigm shifts since its inception: Software 1.0, in which instructions determine behavior, and Software 2.0, in which data determines behavior (machine learning). This paper argues that a third shift - Software 3.0, in which context and reasoning determine behavior - is now underway, and contends that its terminal form converges to three elements: a generalized database (the unified abstraction of all persistent state and memory), a large model (the intelligence core that performs reasoning and generation), and an agent (the execution loop connecting the first two). The core argument is as follows: in the traditional three-tier architecture, the user-interface layer will be absorbed by the model's ability to generate interfaces on demand, the business-logic layer will be re-partitioned along "expressibility x criticality" into model reasoning and storage constraints (with residual deterministic logic retained as tools), and only the data layer will be elevated into the sole persistent infrastructure. We formalize this convergence thesis, present a minimal reference architecture, report evidence from real prototypes and a live model, and systematically analyze both the conditions under which it holds and the boundaries where it fails - determinism, cost, security, and verifiability delimit the thesis's domain of applicability. We argue that the thesis holds in task domains that are expressible, verifiable, externally stateful, and tool-complete, and that it will reshape the roles of developers, the database industry, and the software-engineering discipline.

</details>


### [115] [MemTrapBench: Benchmarking Cognitive Traps in LLM Memory Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20202)
*Mengru Wang,Haozhe Luo,Zhenqian Xu,Zhixiang Cui,Haoming Xu,Qu Yang,Jizhan Fang,Junfeng Fang,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MemTrapBench基准，用于系统评估大语言模型中记忆系统引发的认知陷阱（推理固化和信念扭曲），发现所有现有记忆策略在记忆存在时反而比无记忆设置表现更差（最强者性能下降超10%），并提出AdaptiveMem推理时方法来缓解这些陷阱，同时保持或提升标准记忆基准上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆基准主要评估信息是否被正确提取、存储和检索，却忽略了一个关键问题：检索到的记忆如何重塑模型推理并影响当前任务表现。即使记忆被忠实记录且语义相关，也可能扭曲模型的推理或信念，反而降低当前任务的性能。这种"记忆诱发的认知陷阱"尚未被系统评估。

Method: 本文构建了MemTrapBench基准，涵盖两类认知陷阱：推理固定（Reasoning Fixation）和信念扭曲（Belief Distortion）。同时提出AdaptiveMem，一种简单有效的推理时方法，通过指导LLM避免记忆陷阱来缓解问题。实验横跨两个模型家族和五个代表性记忆框架进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明MemTrapBench对所有评估的记忆策略都具有挑战性：所有记忆策略在此基准上均不如无记忆设置，最强的记忆方法也遭受超过10%的性能下降。AdaptiveMem在MemTrapBench上有效缓解了认知陷阱，同时在多种记忆框架下保持或改善了标准记忆基准的性能。

Conclusion: 记忆系统虽然能帮助LLM保留信息，但可能引入认知陷阱从而损害当前任务表现，这揭示了记忆评估中一个被忽视的重要维度。AdaptiveMem作为一种轻量级推理时方法，能够在不牺牲标准记忆任务性能的前提下缓解这些陷阱，为构建更可靠、更稳健的LLM记忆系统提供了新方向。

Abstract: Memory has become a key component of large language models, enabling them to retain information and learn from long-term interactions. However, existing memory benchmarks mainly evaluate whether information is correctly extracted, stored, and retrieved, while largely overlooking how retrieved memories reshape model reasoning and affect performance on the current task. We identify memory-induced cognitive traps: even faithfully recorded and semantically relevant memories can distort model reasoning or beliefs and degrade current task performance. To systematically evaluate these failure modes, we introduce MemTrapBench, which covers two forms of cognitive traps: Reasoning Fixation and Belief Distortion. Experiments across two model families and five representative memory frameworks show that MemTrapBench is challenging: all evaluated memory strategies underperform the no-memory setting, with even the strongest methods suffering drops of more than 10%. To mitigate these cognitive traps, we propose AdaptiveMem, a simple yet effective inference-time method that instructs LLMs to avoid memory traps. AdaptiveMem mitigates cognitive traps on MemTrapBench while preserving or improving performance on standard memory benchmarks across diverse memory frameworks.

</details>


### [116] [ContractScrub: A benchmark for final review of legal contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20204)
*Yejin Bang,Kirsty Fielding,Brandan Oliver,Brian Birke,Nabeel Seedat,Andrew M. Bean*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ContractScrub，这是首个用于评估LLM合同审校（contract scrubbing）能力的基准测试，由经验丰富的律师手工构建，涵盖多种错误类型。实验发现前沿模型在此任务上表现意外地差，只有最好的模型达到0.75宏平均召回率，尽管它们在通用基准上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合同审校是法律工作中高度适合自动化的任务，具有巨大的经济价值和自动化潜力，但目前没有任何正式的LLM合同审校能力评估基准。尽管人们普遍预期前沿LLM在长上下文推理、一致性检查和命名实体识别等能力上能够很好地适配该任务，但缺乏实际评测数据来验证这一假设。

Method: 作者构建了ContractScrub基准，包含由经验丰富的律师手工设计的合同，覆盖多种错误类别，如定义术语滥用、错误引用、语言不一致等。然后在该基准上评测多个前沿LLM模型的合同审校表现。

Result: 前沿模型在ContractScrub上的表现意外地差，仅有一个模型达到0.75宏平均召回率，尽管这些模型在看起来相关的通用基准上表现强劲。这揭示了当前模型的实际局限。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通用基准的强劲表现并不能保证在专门化领域任务中的良好效果，强调了构建针对性、领域专用的基准来衡量现实世界影响的重要性。

Abstract: Legal work, with its heavy reliance on processing large amounts of text, is often considered one of the domains most exposed to the use of LLMs. Contract ``scrubbing,'' the final review of transactional agreements for errors and inconsistencies, is a particularly suitable task for automation, because it is routine, painstaking work requiring detailed attention to long documents. Scrubbing also seems to align naturally with the general capabilities expected of frontier LLMs around long-context reasoning, consistency checking, and named entity recognition (NER). Despite the economic value and potential for automation, no formal evaluations of LLMs performing contract scrubbing have been conducted. We introduce ContractScrub, the first benchmark designed to evaluate contract scrubbing capabilities, comprising contracts hand-crafted by experienced lawyers over diverse error categories such as misuse of defined terms, incorrect references, and inconsistent language. Frontier models perform surprisingly poorly with only one model reaching 0.75 macro average recall despite strong performance on seemingly related general benchmarks, demonstrating the practical limits of current models and the importance of narrowly targeted, domain-specific benchmarks for measuring real-world impact.

</details>


### [117] [Electronic Navigational Chart Change Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20218)
*Jacob Arndt,Abhishek Potnis,Alexandre Sorokine*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自动化分类电子航海图（ENC）变更的方法，通过将复杂的矢量数据变更编码为结构化表格格式，结合空间上下文编码器和ENC属性编码器，利用梯度提升树在两个运营数据集（1,308对海图，超过100,000条变更）上分别达到90%和94%的准确率，较默认模型提升5-7%，证明了机器学习在ENC维护和海上安全领域的实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水文办公室面临的核心挑战是判断海图变更对海上安全构成关键还是非关键风险。现有工作流依赖人工审查和验证，劳动密集、难以扩展应对大量海图更新，且不同分析人员之间存在判断不一致的问题，迫切需要自动化解决方案。

Method: 提出一种自动分类ENC变更的方法，核心包括：(1) 建立基线编码方案，将复杂矢量数据变更转换为结构化表格格式供分类模型使用；(2) 空间上下文编码器，用于丰富变更表示并融入周围地理特征；(3) ENC属性编码器，用于捕捉修改对象的细微属性值描述。使用具有默认和调优超参数的梯度提升树进行训练和评估。

Result: 在两个不同的运营数据集上（共1,308对海图，含超100,000条独立海图修改），调优的梯度提升树结合所提编码方案分别达到90%和94%的准确率，较使用默认超参数且在无空间上下文和属性嵌入编码上训练的模型有5-7%的提升。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明将机器学习整合到运营地理空间管线中以提高ENC维护效率和增强海上安全是可行的。此外，简单的定位与空间聚合方法的有效性为未来在该应用中评估更复杂的空间表示学习技术奠定了基础。

Abstract: Electronic Navigational Charts (ENCs) are geospatial vector datasets used in maritime navigation systems that represent hydrographic and navigational information such as depths, navigational aids, traffic schemes, and hazards. A major challenge for hydrographic offices is determining whether a given chart change poses a critical or non-critical risk to maritime safety. Existing workflows rely heavily on manual review and verification, which is labor-intensive, scales poorly with the volume of incoming chart updates, and introduces inter-analyst inconsistencies. To address this challenge, we propose a method for automated classification of ENC changes. We establish a baseline encoding scheme to translate complex vector data changes into a structured tabular format for classification models. The two crucial components of the encoding scheme include a spatial context encoder to enrich the change representations with surrounding geographic features, and an ENC attribute encoder to represent nuanced attribute-value descriptions of the modified objects. We evaluate the proposed approach across two distinct operational datasets, comprising 1,308 chart pairs containing over 100,000 individual chart modifications. Tuned gradient-boosted trees leveraging the proposed encoding schemes achieve accuracies of 90% and 94% on the two datasets, yielding a 5-7% improvement over default hyperparameterized models trained on encodings without spatial context and attribute embeddings. These results demonstrate the viability of integrating machine learning into operational geospatial pipelines to improve ENC maintenance and enhance maritime safety. Finally, our experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of simple location and spatial aggregation methods, providing a foundation for evaluating more sophisticated spatial representation learning techniques for this application.

</details>


### [118] [InsufficiencyBench: Evaluating LLM legal advice on underspecified user queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20220)
*Samuel J. Vincent,Daniel Calloway,Fangyi Yu,Andrew M. Bean,Nabeel Seedat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 InsufficiencyBench，这是首个针对"查询侧信息不足"的法律基准，用于评估模型能否识别法律问题中缺失的关键事实、判断缺失内容并避免过早下结论。基准包含8类缺失要素、3种结构性失败模式和202个测试项，实验表明现有前沿模型在此任务上表现很差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的法律AI基准假设查询都是完整明确的，但现实中用户常常省略影响法律结果的关键事实。这一差距导致无法真实评估法律AI系统在实际应用中的可靠性，因此需要专门的基准来衡量模型对查询信息不完整性的识别能力。

Method: 作者形式化定义了8类缺失要素分类法，涵盖三种结构性失败模式（switch、gating、fatal prerequisite），构建了202个基准测试项（58个基础查询和144个缺陷变体），覆盖6个法律领域和24个美国司法辖区，并由执业律师进行标注。

Result: 对10个前沿模型的评估显示，没有模型在缺失要素识别上超过F2=0.46，中位召回率为0.44。模型要么不加区分地进行模糊应答（hedging），要么在虚假预设下静默作答（silent answering）。没有模型能够同时对缺陷查询进行识别并限定回答，同时对完整查询直接作答。

Conclusion: 当前法律AI系统在处理信息不完整的查询时能力严重不足，无法可靠地区分哪些信息是法律上至关重要的缺失。这揭示了现有模型在现实法律场景中应用的重大局限，未来需要在识别查询不完整性方面取得突破。

Abstract: Legal AI systems are increasingly used to answer legal questions, yet existing benchmarks assume queries arrive fully specified. In practice, users omit facts that materially determine the legal outcome. We introduce InsufficiencyBench, the first legal benchmark targeting query-side insufficiency: whether a model recognizes when a query lacks legally material information, identifies what is missing, and refrains from premature conclusions. We formalize a taxonomy of eight canonical missing-element categories across three structural failure modes---switch, gating, and fatal prerequisite--- and construct 202 benchmark items (58 base queries, 144 deficient variants) spanning six legal domains and 24 US jurisdictions and annotated by practising attorneys. Evaluating ten frontier models, we find that no model exceeds F2 = 0.46 on missing-element identification and that the median recall is 0.44. Models either hedge indiscriminately or answer silently under fabricated presumptions. No model both identifies and qualifies responses to deficient queries while directly addressing complete ones.

</details>


### [119] [Rule-Compliant Visual Spatial Planning for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20237)
*Yu Chen,Ting Lei,Yaoyi Li,Jia Cai,Zhecen Wu,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RuleMaze基准测试和DMP方法，用于评估和增强多模态大语言模型在显式或未见规则约束下的视觉空间规划能力。RuleMaze通过可控迷宫导航任务隔离规则合规的空间规划，DMP通过分离感知、执行和规则验证的可解释推理原语来提升规则遵循和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）虽然结合了语言推理与视觉感知，但其在显式或未见过的规则约束下进行视觉空间规划的能力尚未被充分探索。这一任务要求模型同时理解空间布局、解释自然语言规则并规划合规动作，当前缺乏系统化的基准和有效的方法来研究这一能力。

Method: 1) 提出RuleMaze基准，要求MLLMs在遵循不同复杂度自然语言规则的同时导航迷宫。2) 提出Language-Logic-Function Hybridization方法，自动生成自然语言规则并将其转换为逻辑表示和可执行验证器，无需人工规则工程。3) 提出Disentangled Multimodal Planning（DMP）方法，将感知、执行和规则验证分离为可解释的推理原语，从而促进对更复杂和未知规则的系统性泛化。

Result: 实验表明，DMP在规则合规性和规划成功率上显著优于端到端的文本规划基线方法，有效提升了模型在规则约束下的空间规划表现。

Conclusion: RuleMaze为研究MLLMs中基于规则的可解释空间规划提供了原则性基准，DMP通过组件解耦实现了更好的规则遵循与泛化能力，为构建更可解释和可控的多模态规划系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) combine linguistic reasoning with visual perception, yet their ability to perform visual spatial planning under explicit or previously unseen rule constraints remains underexplored. This setting requires models to jointly understand spatial layouts, interpret natural-language rules, and plan valid actions accordingly. To address this gap, we introduce RuleMaze, a controllable benchmark in which MLLMs must navigate mazes while obeying natural-language rules of varying complexity. RuleMaze isolates rule-compliant spatial planning by requiring accurate perception, rule interpretation, and constrained action planning. To enable scalable and systematic rule construction, we propose Language-Logic-Function Hybridization, which automatically generates natural-language rules and translates them into logical representations and executable validators, eliminating manual rule engineering. To improve rule following and generalization, we introduce Disentangled Multimodal Planning (DMP), which separates perception, execution, and rule verification through interpretable reasoning primitives. By disentangling these components, DMP facilitates systematic generalization to more complex and previously unseen rules, while providing transparent intermediate planning traces. Experiments demonstrate that DMP substantially improves rule compliance and planning success compared to end-to-end textual planning baselines. Overall, RuleMaze establishes a principled benchmark for studying grounded and interpretable rule-based spatial planning in MLLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/oceanflowlab/RuleMaze.

</details>


### [120] [QUASAR: A Quantum-Classical Neural Network for SAR Satellite Physical-Layer Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20240)
*Vincenzo Sammartino,Nathanael Denis,Roberto Di Pietro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QUASAR是首个将CNN频谱图编码器与变分量子电路（VQC）融合的量子-经典混合架构，用于X波段SAR卫星信号的物理层认证（PLA）。它相比经典机器学习更高效，仅需10%训练数据即可匹配经典基线精度；在相同数据预算下分类精度更优，并在三种对抗场景（重放、伪造IQ注入、星载欺骗）中分别以89.7%、94.1%和81.3%的拒绝率验证了其有效性，开创了量子增强物理层分类器的先河。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X波段SAR卫星在灾害响应、环境监测和军事侦察中至关重要，但缺乏鲁棒的物理层认证（PLA）。现有PLA系统主要基于射频指纹识别，受限于sub-6 GHz频段且依赖经典深度学习，无法充分捕捉卫星硬件中区分性的IQ相位非线性特征，因此需要一种能在X波段提供有效PLA的新方案。

Method: QUASAR采用量子-经典混合架构：使用CNN频谱图编码器提取X波段SAR信号的频谱特征，然后将其输入变分量子电路（VQC）进行分类。该方法通过VQC的量子表达能力增强对IQ相位非线性特征的建模能力，实现对卫星硬件身份的有效识别与认证。

Result: 在三种对抗场景中，QUASAR对欺骗传输的拒绝率分别为：重放攻击89.7%、伪造IQ注入94.1%、星载欺骗81.3%。在数据效率方面，仅需经典基线10%的训练数据即可达到相同精度；在同等数据预算下，分类准确率优于经典基线。

Conclusion: QUASAR首次证明了量子增强方案在卫星星座物理层认证中的可行性，为X波段SAR卫星提供了正交于密码学的安全层，并开辟了物理层认证研究的新方向。完整的框架与实验结果展示了量子机器学习在安全通信领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: X-band SAR satellites (8-12 GHz) play a critical role in disaster response, environmental monitoring, and military intelligence. Yet, they lack robust physical-layer authentication (PLA), a security layer orthogonal to cryptographic solutions. Existing PLA systems, typically based on radio-frequency fingerprinting, are often limited to sub-6 GHz frequencies and rely on classical deep learning. However, this approach underfits the IQ phase nonlinearities that distinguish satellite hardware. In this paper, we present QUASAR, to the best of our knowledge the first quantum-classical hybrid architecture that fuses a CNN spectrogram encoder with a variational quantum circuit (VQC) to provide PLA to X-band SAR signals. Our solution enjoys two distinctive features: (i) it is markedly more data-efficient than classical machine learning, requiring only 10% of the training data to match the accuracy of classical baselines -- data collection being notoriously the most time-consuming phase of PLA; and, (ii) at an equal data budget, it improves classification accuracy over those baselines. In detail, we test our solution under three adversarial scenarios: replay, crafted-IQ injection, and space-borne spoofing. QUASAR rejects spoofed transmissions in 89.7%, 94.1%, and 81.3% of attempts, respectively, establishing the first quantum-enhanced physical-layer classifier for satellite constellations. The fully detailed framework and the supporting results, other than being interesting on their own, show a novel research avenue for physical-layer authentication.

</details>


### [121] [Learning When to Think: Adaptive Reasoning for Test-Time Compute Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20256)
*Gijs Kassenaar,Zhao Yang,Vincent François-Lavet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出让推理语言模型通过自主选择三种推理模式（NoThink、Short、Long）来自适应分配推理计算量。在GRPO中学习模式选择，无需独立的路由器。在1.5B数学模型上，三种模式成功涌现且未塌缩，将平均响应长度从4,796削减至2,811个token（减少41%），同时保持接近基线的准确率，并能在其他基准上零训练迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推理语言模型通常在固定token预算下运行，而非自适应地分配计算资源，导致简单问题上过度计算、困难问题上计算不足。作者希望让模型自己学会按问题难度分配推理强度。

Method: 让模型在响应首token选择三种模式之一：NoThink（快速作答）、Short（简短推理）、Long（扩展推理）。在GRPO中通过奖励塑形（shaped reward）使每种模式在特定响应长度下都有价值，同时对每种模式设定硬性的token上限以保持模式间的区分，无需额外路由器。

Result: 在1.5B蒸馏模型和MATH数据集上，三种模式成功涌现且未塌缩为单一选择。简短模式比Long模式更准确，说明路由器按难度而非随机分类问题。三重种子平均后，MATH500上准确率从0.796略降至0.782，但平均响应长度从4,796降至2,811 token（降低41%）。在其他基准上无需重新训练即可迁移，GSM8K上节省76%的token且精度更高。

Conclusion: 构建了一个能按问题难度自适应选择推理量的推理模型，实现计算效率与精度的良好权衡，并展现出跨基准的泛化能力。

Abstract: Reasoning language models trained with reinforcement learning typically operate under a fixed token budget rather than an explicitly adaptive one, which can lead to over-computation on easy problems and insufficient computation on difficult ones. We study whether a model can learn to allocate its own reasoning effort by choosing, as the first token of its response, one of three modes: \textsc{NoThink} (answer as quickly as possible), \textsc{Short} (brief reasoning), or \textsc{Long} (extended reasoning). The choice is learned inside Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with no separate router, through a shaped reward that makes each mode worthwhile at a different response length, together with hard per-mode token caps that keep the modes distinct. On a 1.5B distilled model trained on MATH, the three modes emerge without collapsing to a single choice, and the brief modes end up more accurate than \textsc{Long}, which shows that the router sorts problems by difficulty rather than at random. Averaged over three seeds, the resulting policy stays close to the base model's accuracy on the held-out MATH500 ($0.782$ vs.\ $0.796$) while cutting the mean response length from $4{,}796$ to $2{,}811$ tokens (a $41\%$ reduction). Interestingly, it also transfers to other benchmarks without retraining, with the largest savings where problems are easier, with for instance 76\% token reduction on GSM8K and at higher accuracy than the baselines at similar response length. In short, we build a reasoning model that adaptively chooses how much to reason for each problem.

</details>


### [122] [Catching the Rug: Early Prediction of Fraudulent Memecoins on Solana via Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20271)
*Jianghai Li,Pavel Kuznetsov,Yury Yanovich,Konstantin Nott-Whaley,Igor Vodolazov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次在Solana生态系统中进行大规模meme币Rug Pull早期检测研究，构建了包含640万代币、覆盖7个月的数据集。研究发现绝大多数meme币在发布一小时内就表现出Rug Pull特征，使用仅前5分钟交易数据的经典机器学习模型（特别是XGBoost）即可实现稳健检测；同时验证了PumpFun与Raydium之间的跨平台泛化能力，发现多源数据融合能有效缓解域偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: meme币在区块链平台上的快速增长增加了诈骗（特别是Rug Pull）风险。以往研究集中在以太坊上的代币，而Solana作为meme币交易量和代币数量的领先区块链却缺乏系统研究。且Solana上的Rug Pull机制与以太坊不同（主要依赖流动性操纵和社会动态而非智能合约后门），需要专门的检测方案。

Method: 构建了包含640万代币、覆盖7个月的Solana大型数据集；分析Rug Pull的市场特征和时序规律；使用仅包含前5分钟交易数据的经典机器学习模型（重点为XGBoost）进行短时预测；评估PumpFun与Raydium之间的跨平台泛化，并采用多源数据融合策略缓解域偏移。

Result: 市场分析显示绝大多数meme币在发布一小时内即呈现Rug Pull特征；在无代码级特征的情况下，XGBoost等经典机器学习模型仅凭前5分钟交易数据即可实现稳健的Rug Pull检测；多源数据融合显著提升了跨平台检测的可靠性，有效缓解了域偏移问题。

Conclusion: 该研究推进了对高吞吐量链上DeFi欺诈的理解，为保护投资者提供了实用的早期检测框架，并验证了数据融合策略在跨平台场景下的有效性。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of memecoins on blockchain platforms has increased the risk of fraudulent activities, particularly rug pulls. While previous studies have focused on Ethereum-based tokens, this paper shifts the spotlight to Solana, the leading blockchain for memecoins by trading volume and token count. Unlike Ethereum, where rug pulls often exploit smart contract backdoors, Solana memecoin rug pulls are predominantly driven by liquidity manipulation and social dynamics. This research pioneers large-scale rug pull early detection in the Solana ecosystem by assembling a dataset of 6.4 million tokens over 7 months. Market analysis reveals that a vast majority of these memecoins exhibit rug pull characteristics within one hour of launch, highlighting the urgency of short-horizon prediction. Despite the absence of code-level features, we demonstrate that classic machine learning models, particularly Gradient Boosting (XGBoost), achieve robust performance in detecting potential rug pulls using only the first 5 minutes of trading data. Furthermore, we evaluate cross-platform generalization between PumpFun and Raydium, revealing that multi-source data fusion significantly mitigates domain shift and improves detection reliability. This study advances the understanding of DeFi fraud on high-throughput chains and provides a practical framework for protecting investors.

</details>


### [123] [Break It Down, Pass It On: Cross-Task Skill Transfer in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20274)
*Yiyang Feng,Biddut Sarker Bijoy,Niranjan Balasubramanian,Jiawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM代理中技能归纳方式如何影响其跨任务迁移效果，发现子任务级技能优于任务级技能，文本格式技能优于代码格式技能；并提出一个"技能效用分数"（结合特异性与抽象性）作为无需任务执行即可预测技能迁移表现的实用诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理可以从已完成任务中归纳技能并复用以提升能力，但实际归纳的技能可能迁移不可靠甚至有害。此前缺乏对"技能归纳方式如何决定其跨任务迁移可靠性"的系统性理解。

Method: 进行综合对照实验，沿两个维度比较技能归纳方式：（1）任务级vs子任务级技能归纳；（2）文本vs代码技能格式。进一步提出两个互补属性——特异性（技能与真实任务的匹配度）和抽象性（技能相关性在不同任务间的均匀程度），并组合成技能效用分数作为预测指标。

Result: 任务级技能大多使代理性能低于无记忆基线，而子任务级技能使性能平均高于基线；文本技能迁移优于代码技能。单独的特异性或抽象性都不能预测任务成功，但两者组合（技能效用分数）与技能迁移时的任务成功一致相关，且子任务级和文本技能得分更高。

Conclusion: 技能效用分数仅需技能和任务描述即可计算，无需任务执行，因此可作为任何新任务运行前的技能记忆实用诊断工具，为构建更可靠的LLM代理技能复用机制提供指导。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can induce skills from completed tasks and reuse them later to grow more capable with experience. In practice, induced skills may transfer unreliably and can even harm the agent that retrieves them. When agent-induced skills transfer reliably across tasks remains an open question. We conduct a comprehensive and controlled study of how the way skills are induced shapes their transfer across tasks. Specifically, we compare task-level with subtask-level skill induction and text with code skill formats, the two axes along which existing methods differ. Task-level skills mostly reduce the agent's performance below its no-memory baseline while subtask-level skills raise it above on average, and text skills transfer better than code skills. To further understand our findings, we examine two complementary properties of the induced skills: specificity, which measures how closely a skill matches real tasks, and abstractness, which measures how evenly its relevance spreads across tasks. Neither property alone predicts task success, but their combined effect does, which we propose as a skill utility score. The score correlates consistently with task success when skills are transferred, and subtask-level and text skills score higher. Computing skill utility only needs the skills and task descriptions but not any task execution, so our score serves as a practical diagnostic of a skill memory before any new task runs.

</details>


### [124] [Phantom Gains: Auditing Self-Improvement Against a Measured Null](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20290)
*Cheng Xu,Nan Yan,Liming Chen,M-Tahar Kechadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出现有语言模型自我提升评估方式（逐问题得失追踪）因两次噪声估计作差而产生测量伪影，并通过三轮回LoRA自训练对照实验识别出七种测量失败，提出基于FDR控制的逐问题精确检验法替代原有统计量。该审计方法利用多臂研究中已有的基线重复数据构建零分布，无需新增实验，严格区分了外部蒸馏与自训练的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型是否自我提升的评价标准正从平均准确率转向逐问题得失的追踪，但这种追踪需要对两个噪声估计作差，容易受测量伪影干扰，导致报告结论被反转。现有统计方法缺乏独立的零假设检验，无法可靠地区分真实能力变化与测量噪声。

Method: 对Qwen3-8B进行三轮回rank-32 LoRA自训练，与经过相同流水线的冻结控制组对照，识别七类测量失败。提出用逐问题精确检验对合并基线进行FDR控制，替代原有的扩增统计量与自然阈值修复。同时采用多种臂（多任务）在匹配流、体量和评估下的阶梯式对比，并通过回归检验验证结论。

Result: 逐问题精确检验在受检副本上均未检测到异常，且对多重检验规则、错误率和合并规模均保持稳健。外部蒸馏改善了基线模型难以触及的问题，而三种自训练形式均未做到，回归检验排除了这是蒸馏更大总体增益的副产品（p<10⁻⁸）。对于基线模型从未触及的更小问题集，证据尚不明确；自训练对基线已解决问题集的破坏率高于实测下限。

Conclusion: 转换级审计要求对每个报告的统计量配备单独测量得到的零假设，这些零假设无需新实验，可以从多臂研究已有的基线重复中构建，但需要足够多的重复样本。标准实践中的若干统计方法存在系统性缺陷，需要替换为更严格的控制假设检验。

Abstract: Whether a language model has improved itself is increasingly judged not by mean accuracy but by which individual problems it gains and loses. Tracking these transitions means differencing two noisy estimates, leaving them vulnerable to measurement artifacts. Auditing three rounds of rank-$32$ LoRA self-training on Qwen3-8B against a frozen control pushed through the identical pipeline, we identify seven measurement failures, each of which inverts a reported finding when its control is absent. Several are standard practice. A ledger built on a single greedy decode manufactures capability changes on an untrained model, largely an artifact of inference batching; the expansion statistic separating acquisition from sharpening assigns that same model a rate of $0.280$. The natural threshold repair does not survive replication: estimated across the frozen comparisons such a design already contains, its null stays non-zero. We replace it with a per-problem exact test against a pooled baseline under false-discovery-rate control, which detects nothing on any held-out replicate and is unchanged under the multiple-testing rule, error rate and pool size. Applied to a ladder of arms matched in stream, volume and evaluation, the audit finds that external distillation improves problems the base model rarely reaches while three forms of self-training do not; a regression rejects this asymmetry as a by-product of distillation's larger overall gain ($p < 10^{-8}$). On the far smaller set of problems the base model never reaches, the evidence is inconclusive, while self-training corrupts problems solved at baseline at rates well above the measured floor. Transition-level auditing therefore requires a separately measured null for every statistic it reports: nulls that cost no new experiments, built from baseline replicates a multi-arm study already owns, though not from as few as most possess.

</details>


### [125] [MidTool: Mid-training Data Synthesis for Agentic Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20314)
*Fengqing Jiang,Yite Wang,Boyi Liu,Zhaoyang Wang,Canwen Xu,Zhewei Yao,Radha Poovendran,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MidTool，一个用于智能体工具使用中间训练的开源语料构建流水线，结合大规模Web、PDF和代码数据与来自真实工具API、MCP技能和文档驱动工作流的合成监督信号。在Qwen3-4B/8B-Base上中间训练后，配合SFT和RL后训练，在BFCL、tau2-Bench和MCP Universe基准上持续提升下游性能，证明通用工具使用能力需要专门的中间训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明定向的中间训练能增强数学、科学等推理密集型能力及软件工程场景中的智能体能力，但并行且较少被探索的通用工具使用这一智能体能力尚未得到充分研究。作者旨在探索工具使用能力是否同样受益于专门的中间训练阶段。

Method: 提出MidTool开源语料构建流水线，整合大规模Web、PDF与代码数据，并从真实工具API、MCP技能和文档驱动工作流中合成监督数据。该数据集设计用于教会模型识别工具可用性（tool affordance）、从上下文中接地参数、组合工具调用工作流以及从不完整信息中恢复。作者在MidTool-Mix上对Qwen3-4B-Base和Qwen3-8B-Base进行中间训练，随后分别应用SFT和RL两种后训练策略。

Result: 与基线相比，MidTool-Mix在SFT和RL两种后训练设置下均在BFCL、tau2-Bench和MCP Universe基准上一致地提升了下游性能。

Conclusion: 通用工具使用与其他重要的大语言模型能力一样，受益于专门的中间训练阶段，而非完全留给后训练阶段处理。

Abstract: Mid-training is increasingly recognized as a critical stage for shaping the capabilities of large language models. Recent work has shown that targeted mid-training can strengthen reasoning-intensive abilities such as math and science, and can also improve agentic capabilities in software-engineering settings. In this work, we study the parallel but less explored agentic capability: general tool use. We present MidTool, an open corpus construction pipeline for agentic tool-use mid-training that combines large-scale web, PDF, and code data with synthesized supervision from real-world tool APIs, MCP skills, and document-grounded workflows. MidTool is designed to teach models how to recognize tool affordances, ground arguments from context, compose tool call workflow, and recover from incomplete information. We mid-train Qwen3-4B-Base and Qwen3-8B-Base on MidTool-Mix, and then apply follow-up post-training with both supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. Compared with baselines, MidTool-Mix consistently improves downstream performance under both SFT and RL on BFCL, tau2-Bench, and MCP Universe. These results suggest that general tool use, like other important LLM capabilities, benefits from dedicated mid-training rather than being left entirely to post-training.

</details>


### [126] [Pandora's AI Model Routing Box: Efficient Allocation with Costly Value Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20316)
*Adam Fisch,Shubhendu Trivedi,Fantine Huot,William W. Cohen,Michael Kaisers,Mirella Lapata,Kate Larson,Jacob Eisenstein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Pandora's Router与Pandora's Bidder两种路由策略，将异构AI系统中"评估专业模型价值"的成本-质量权衡形式化为Pandora's Box问题（经典的有代价检查最优搜索问题），在高斯信号模型下推导出闭合形式的信息价值表达式，从而在集中式与去中心化场景中决定是否值得为每个查询投资价值估计。实验表明该方法在匹配详尽估计路由质量的同时大幅减少昂贵估计器的调用频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构AI系统（由多个模型、架构、推理设置组成）通过将查询路由给最合适且成本最低的专家模型来提升质量与效率。但路由需要估计每个专家的预期回报，而估计本身有成本：廉价的估计器（如基于嵌入的预测器）快速但噪声大，精确的估计器（如微调模型结合检索结果或部分推理轨迹）昂贵但准确。需要在估计成本与路由质量之间做最优权衡。

Method: 将"牺牲估计成本换取更优路由决策"的权衡形式化为Pandora's Box问题。在高斯信号模型假设下，得到闭合形式的信息价值（value-of-information）表达式，据此确定对每个专家和输入是否值得细化价值估计。集中式方案为Pandora's Router；去中心化方案为Pandora's Bidder，其中各专家独立决定是否投资自评估，再接受报价认领查询。实验覆盖三领域：多LLM基准、检索增强专家、可变推理时长的LLM。

Result: 集中式Pandora's Router能匹配穷举估计的路由质量，同时大幅减少昂贵估计器的查询次数。去中心化场景中，当竞争估计准确时，信息价值推理提升配置效率；当竞争估计噪声大时，策略性专家可借此提升自身效用而损害他人。

Conclusion: Pandora's Box框架为异构AI系统中的专家价值估计成本提供了理论最优的决策原则，集中式场景下效果显著；但去中心化场景中需警惕信息价值推理在市场参与者估计噪声较大时可能带来的策略性滥用与效率损失。

Abstract: Heterogeneous AI systems composed of multiple models, architectures, harnesses, or inference-time settings can improve quality and efficiency by routing queries to the specialist who can answer most effectively at the lowest cost. Routing requires estimating each specialist's expected return, but this value estimation has a cost. Cheap estimators (e.g., embedding-based predictors) are fast but noisy, while accurate estimators (e.g., fine-tuned models with access to retrieval results or partial reasoning traces) are expensive. We formalize this tradeoff as an instance of Pandora's Box, the classical problem of optimal search with costly inspection. Under a Gaussian signal model, the resulting policies have closed-form value-of-information expressions that determine, for each specialist and input, whether refining the value estimate is worth its cost. We call the centralized policy Pandora's Router. We extend this to a decentralized setting, Pandora's Bidder, where specialists independently decide whether to invest in self-assessment before accepting an offered price to claim a query. Experiments across three domains---a standard multi-LLM benchmark, retrieval-augmented specialists, and LLMs with variable inference-time reasoning---show that Pandora's Router matches the routing quality of exhaustive estimation, while querying the expensive estimator far less often. In the decentralized setting, value-of-information reasoning improves allocative efficiency when competing estimates are accurate; when competing estimates are noisy, however, it can increase the strategic specialist's utility at the expense of others.

</details>


### [127] [AI4AI-Bench: Benchmarking LLM Agents in Algorithmic Design for Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20318)
*Yizhe Chi,Wenyi Li,Deyao Hong,Xiaoqiu Wang,Mingju Gao,Kaisen Yang,Bingxiang He,Youjie Zheng,Calvin Xiao,Qinhuai Na*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI4AI-Bench，一个包含10个冻结研究仓库、覆盖10种训练算法家族的基准套件，用于单独衡量AI系统设计训练算法的能力（递归自我改进的核心前提）。在29个配置×6个系统的测试中，平均得分仅0.166，最强系统也只达到0.250（关闭了仓库已有算法与最优之间不到五分之一的距离）。分析显示多数提交根本不改变模型学习方式，而投入更多推理努力能显著提升尝试改变算法比例的倾向与得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进（RSI）的可行性取决于AI系统能否设计训练算法（改进目标函数或更新规则以提升算力-能力交换率）。但现有基准无法独立衡量这一能力：已有套件要么靠收集数据取胜，要么只是调超参数，且都无法区分'改变运行执行方式'与'改变模型学习方式'的区别。因此需要一个能精确隔离并衡量训练算法设计能力的基准。

Method: 构建AI4AI-Bench：10个冻结的研究仓库，覆盖10种训练算法家族。每个任务中，智能体有4小时在一台B300上重写训练算法，其代码随后从头重新运行最多12小时，由对智能体隐藏的固定评估器评分，与同一流程下仓库原始算法进行比较。由于10个指标不可通约，每个任务映射到统一尺度：0为无信息模型，0.1为仓库原始算法，1.0为任务最优。

Result: 在29个配置、6个系统、10个任务上的平均得分为0.166，最强系统达到0.250，即最强系统也仅关闭了仓库已有算法与最优间不到1/5的差距。大多数提交从不改变模型学习方式（这些平均0.126），少数真正改变学习方式的提交平均0.226。更多推理努力将改变学习方式的提交比例从8%提升到64%，平均得分从0.094提升到0.196。

Conclusion: 当前最强的AI系统在训练算法设计上能力依然薄弱，距离RSI可行所需的能力仍有很大差距。推理努力的增加主要带来的是'愿意去改变学习算法'的倾向而非实质性的算法改进质量。作者开源了任务套件、评估器和所有打分提交，以便随系统发展可重复测量该能力。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) asks whether an AI system can improve the process that produces AI systems, so that the next system inherits the improvement. That process is the training algorithm: a better objective or update rule improves the compute\mbox{-}capability exchange rate for every subsequent run, including the one that produces the next agent. Whether RSI is feasible therefore turns on whether an agent can design training algorithms. No benchmark isolates that ability: existing suites are won by collecting data or by tuning hyperparameters, and none tells a change to how a run is executed apart from a change to how the model learns. We present AI4AI\mbox{-}Bench, 10 frozen research repositories spanning 10 training algorithm families. In each task, an agent has 4 hours on one B300 to rewrite the training algorithm; its code is then rerun from scratch for up to 12 hours and scored by a fixed evaluator hidden from the agent, against the repository's original algorithm under the same procedure. Because the 10 metrics are incommensurable, every task is mapped onto one scale on which $0$ is an uninformative model, $0.1$ is the algorithm the repository ships, and $1.0$ is the task optimum. Across 29 configurations of 6 systems on all 10 tasks the mean score is $0.166$, and the best system reaches $0.250$: even the strongest closes under a fifth of the distance between the algorithm that was already there and the optimum. The submissions show where that distance went: most never change how the model learns at all, and the minority that do average $0.226$ against $0.126$ for the rest. More reasoning effort mostly buys the willingness to go there, taking that minority from $8\%$ of submissions to $64\%$ and the mean score from $0.094$ to $0.196$. We release the task suite, the evaluators and every scored submission, so that the measurement can be repeated as these systems change.

</details>


### [128] [An Agentic Approach for Active Data Collection, Travel Behavior Modeling, and Weather-Sensitive Demand Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20320)
*Narges Ahmadi,Yubo Jiao,Jônatas Augusto Manzolli,Jiangbo Yu,Luis Miranda-Moreno*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出一个三智能体工作流，将对话式数据收集、结构化数据处理和行为预测整合起来。通过聊天机器人和天气图像增强的陈述偏好调查收集454个学生通勤者的模式选择数据，评估了多项logit模型、随机森林以及9个本地部署的大语言模型（2-350亿参数）。随机森林达到69.6%的五分类准确率，最优零样本纯文本LLM达到69.9%，而视觉增强配置达到71.5%，表明多智能体协调工作流在行为预测中具有潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 出行行为研究常常将数据收集与预测建模分开开发和评估，缺乏端到端的整合。该研究旨在弥合这一鸿沟，提出一个可审计的多智能体工作流，将对话式调查数据收集、结构化数据处理和多模态行为预测协调起来，探索LLM在该领域的应用价值。

Method: 采用三智能体工作流：(1) 聊天机器人管理、图像增强的陈述偏好调查，在五种天气场景下收集学生通勤者的出行方式选择；(2) 使用多项logit模型分析天气关联，以逻辑回归和随机森林作为机器学习基准；(3) 评估9个本地部署的LLM（2-350亿参数），涵盖四种零样本提示条件，并扩展至人物角色、少样本和基于视觉的配置。

Result: 随机森林达到69.6%五分类准确率；最佳纯文本零样本LLM达到69.9%无需任务特定拟合；习惯性出行信息带来最一致的性能提升；专家框架优于角色扮演；人物角色信息在缺乏习惯性出行信息时最有用；少样本提示对多个模型有改进且增益在少量示例后趋于稳定；最佳视觉配置达到71.5%的五分类准确率。

Conclusion: 研究表明对话式调查、结构化数据处理、传统行为建模、机器学习与多模态LLM预测可以在一个可审计的多智能体工作流中协调运作。视觉上下文可为特定模型提供额外预测信息，且本地部署的LLM在零样本条件下即可达到与传统机器学习模型相当的性能。

Abstract: Travel behavior research increasingly combines digital data collection with predictive modeling, yet these stages are often developed and evaluated separately. This study proposes a three-agent workflow integrating conversational data collection, structured data processing, and behavioral prediction. A chatbot-administered, image-augmented stated-preference survey collected mode choices from student commuters across five predefined weather scenarios, yielding 454 respondent-scenario observations. Weather-related associations were analyzed using a multinomial logit model, while logistic regression and random forest provided machine-learning benchmarks. Nine locally deployed large language models (LLMs), ranging from 2 to 35 billion parameters, were evaluated across four zero-shot prompt-and-context conditions and extended through persona, few-shot, and vision-based configurations. Random forest achieved 69.6% five-class accuracy, while the best text-only zero-shot LLM reached 69.9% without task-specific fitting. Habitual travel information produced the most consistent gains, Expert framing generally outperformed Role-Play, and persona information was most useful when habitual travel information was unavailable. Few-shot prompting improved prediction for several models, with gains stabilizing after a small number of examples. Using the same weather images shown to respondents, the best vision-based configuration reached 71.5% five-class accuracy, indicating that visual context may provide additional predictive information for selected models. Overall, the study shows how conversational surveys, structured data processing, conventional behavioral modeling, machine learning, and multimodal LLM prediction can be coordinated within an auditable multi-agent workflow.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [APPROVE: Visual End-User-in-the-Loop Robot Programming with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19281)
*Bijan Kavousian,Miray Özakkas,Josefine Monnet,Oliver Petrovic,Christian Brecher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: APPROVE是一个基于LLM的多模态端用户机器人编程框架，集成了自然语言输入、Blockly块状界面和显式用户确认步骤，允许用户在程序执行前进行确认、修改或拒绝，并将确认的功能存入库中以供复用，从而提升透明性、意图对齐和可重用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人编程需要专家知识，即使基于块的界面也缺乏灵活性；现有的LLM自动生成机器人程序方法存在缺乏透明性、无法确保与用户意图对齐、以及难以复用的局限。

Method: APPROVE框架将自然语言输入与基于块的界面（Blockly）和显式用户确认步骤相结合。LLM生成的程序被可视化于Blockly块状界面中，用户可在执行前确认、修改或拒绝；已确认的功能存入库中供后续复用，逐步积累可靠的程序组件库。

Result: 该框架贡献了一种以人为中心的LLM机器人编程设计方法，强调用户信任、意图对齐和可重用性三个核心维度。

Conclusion: 通过引入显式用户确认与可视化反馈机制，APPROVE有效弥补了现有LLM程序生成方法在透明度和用户控制方面的不足，并通过组件库实现逐步积累和复用，为非专家用户提供了一种更可靠、可信的机器人编程途径。

Abstract: Programming robots remains challenging for non-experts, as traditional methods require expert knowledge and even block-based interfaces often lack flexibility. Recent work has explored Large Language Models (LLMs) to automatically generate robot programs from natural language, but these systems remain limited by a lack of transparency, missing mechanisms to ensure alignment with user intent, and little support for reuse. We present APPROVE (AI-Powered Programming for Robots with Visual End-User Feedback), an LLM-based multi-modal end-user programming framework that integrates natural language input with a block-based interface and an explicit user confirmation step. Generated programs are visualized using a block-based interface in Blockly, allowing users to confirm, modify, or reject them before execution. Confirmed functions are stored in a library for reuse, gradually building a set of reliable program components. Our approach contributes a human-centered design for LLM-based robot programming that emphasizes user trust, intent alignment, and reusability.

</details>


### [130] [The Missing Touch: Spatially Distributed Tactile Feedback Brings Teleoperation Closer to Human Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19372)
*Rohan Kota,Gregory Reardon,J. Edward Colgate*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发现机器人遥操作性能与人类灵巧度差距的关键因素在于缺乏空间分布式的触觉反馈。通过使用配备32自由度指尖触觉显示器与双自由度力反馈遥操作机械臂，研究表明当远程操作端的局部形变被忠实还原到操作者指尖时，操作性能显著提升，不仅加速了任务完成，还将遥操作轨迹与自然人类轨迹的偏差减少了29-79%。此外，提高触觉反馈的分辨率压缩了遥操作运动的状态空间分布，有利于训练自主机器人策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作被广泛用于收集训练自主机器人策略的演示数据，但遥操作机器人的性能仍远低于人类灵巧度，即使对于基本任务也是如此。先前研究未充分揭示导致这一性能差距的关键因素，本文旨在验证空间分布式触觉反馈的缺失是否是核心原因。

Method: 使用一个双自由度（DoF）双边力反馈遥操作机械臂，配备32自由度指尖触觉显示器。在遥操作任务中，忠实地将远程操作端的局部形变还原到操作者指尖，并进行一系列对照实验，同时通过调整位移量化的精细程度来控制触觉反馈的分辨率，比较不同条件下的任务表现。

Result: 再现分布式接触信息不仅加速了任务性能，还通过最小化纠正动作和任务完成步骤，将遥操作轨迹与自然人类轨迹的偏差减少了29-79%。提高触觉反馈分辨率（细化位移量化粒度）压缩了遥操作运动的状态空间分布，这与改善自主机器人策略的训练效果相关。

Conclusion: 空间分布式触觉反馈对于弥合人类与遥操作灵巧度之间的差距至关重要，也是训练下一代自主机器人的关键要素。

Abstract: A fundamental challenge in robotic teleoperation is enabling an operator to control a remote robot as effortlessly and intuitively as their own hands. Despite the growing use of teleoperation to collect demonstration data for training autonomous robot policies, teleoperated robot performance still falls significantly short of human dexterity, even for basic tasks. Here, we present evidence that a key factor contributing to this performance gap is the absence of spatially distributed tactile feedback. Using a two-degree-of-freedom (DoF) bilateral force-feedback telemanipulator paired with a 32-DoF tactile fingertip display, we show that operator performance improves significantly when localized deformations on the remote manipulator are faithfully reproduced on the operator's fingertip. In a series of teleoperation tasks, reproducing distributed contact information not only accelerated task performance but also brought teleoperated movements closer to natural human behavior by minimizing corrective actions and task completion steps, thereby reducing the deviation between teleoperated and natural trajectories by 29$\unicode{x2013}$79%. Furthermore, we found that increasing the resolution of the tactile feedback$\unicode{x2014}$by refining how finely the measured displacements were quantized for reproduction$\unicode{x2014}$compressed the state-space distribution of teleoperated motions, which has been associated with improved training outcomes for autonomous robot policies. Together, these results suggest that spatially distributed tactile feedback is essential for closing the gap between human and teleoperated dexterity and training the next generation of autonomous robots.

</details>


### [131] [Learning the Right Abstraction: Neural Reduced Dynamics for Complex Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19375)
*Harry Zhang,Dan Negrut*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出神经约简动力学（NRD）框架，通过学习保留控制相关物理的约简状态，在冻结的神经网络模型中训练策略，再迁移回高保真仿真器验证。在履带车辆轨迹跟踪和机械臂目标到达等任务中，NRD模型比高保真仿真快约4个数量级，所有策略均成功转移且表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真具身AI仿真器计算成本高昂，难以直接用于大规模强化学习。作者主张使用精度较低但速度更快的仿真（如数据驱动的神经动力学模型），其核心价值在于学习正确的抽象——即保留控制相关物理的约简状态，从而在不牺牲策略质量的前提下实现高通量策略学习。

Method: 开发神经约简动力学（NRD）框架，其关键思想是分离模型传播的状态与可作为输入供给或解析恢复的状态；策略完全在冻结的学习模型内部训练，然后在高保真仿真器中验证。通过两个案例研究（地形感知的HMMWV轨迹跟踪，以及履带车辆及其前装铰接臂的目标到达）在三个控制任务上验证该框架。

Result: 所有策略均成功迁移回高保真仿真器。一个在地形条件动力学模型中训练且自身无地形输入的策略，在三种地形上的中位和平均跟踪误差均低于单地形专业策略（包括零样本崎岖地形）。履带车辆达到100/100目标，机械臂97/100目标，零接触或关节限位违规。NRD模型比高保真仿真快约4个数量级。

Conclusion: 神经约简动力学可作为精确但昂贵的物理仿真与可扩展机器人学习之间的桥梁，使迭代式在线策略学习变得实用，同时保证策略在高保真场景中的可迁移性和可靠性。

Abstract: High-fidelity embodied AI simulators provide realistic evaluation of complex robotic systems, but their computational cost limits their direct use for large-scale reinforcement learning campaigns. We advocate the use of less accurate but more expeditious simulations, which might draw on data-driven, e.g., neural dynamics, models. This contribution argues that the practical value of a neural dynamics model for complex robot control lies in learning the \emph{right abstraction}: a reduced state that preserves the control-relevant physics of the high-fidelity system while enabling high-throughput policy learning. We develop a neural reduced dynamics (NRD) framework that separates the state the model propagates from what can be supplied as an input or recovered analytically, trains policies entirely inside the frozen learned model, and validates them back in the high-fidelity simulator. Two case studies instantiate it across three control tasks: terrain-aware HMMWV trajectory tracking on rigid, bumpy and deformable Continuum Representation Model (CRM) terrain; and goal reaching for a stock tracked vehicle and its front-mounted articulated arm. Every policy transfers back to the high-fidelity simulator. A single policy trained inside the terrain-conditioned dynamics model, and given no terrain input of its own, attains lower median and mean tracking error than both single-terrain specialists on all three terrains, including zero-shot bumpy terrain. Quantitatively, the tracked vehicle reaches 100 of 100 goals and the arm 97 of 100, with zero contacts or joint-limit violations. The NRD models advance roughly four orders of magnitude faster in simulated time than the high-fidelity simulator scenes they replace, making iterative on-policy learning practical and supporting neural reduced dynamics as a bridge between accurate but expensive physics simulation and scalable robot learning.

</details>


### [132] [SCAPE: Scenario-Conditioned Simulation-Augmented Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19425)
*Dijie Zhu,Seunghun Oh,Ruopeng Huang,Zhiyu Huang,Jiaqi Ma,Chen Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCAPE是一个场景条件化的仿真增强策略评估框架，利用有限的配对仿真-真实样本和大规模仿真rollouts来预测真实世界策略性能，并修正仿真到真实的偏差和校准预测不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的仿真增强评估方法仅关注初始条件和部署设置上的平均性能，掩盖了场景特定变化，对策略何时何地可安全部署缺乏指导。真实环境测试成本高难以扩展，仿真测试存在sim-to-real偏差，需要更精细的场景级性能预测。

Method: SCAPE框架包含三个关键组件：1) 利用有限的配对仿真-真实数据修正仿真标签中的sim-to-real偏差；2) 基于修正后的标签和大规模仿真rollouts训练场景条件化的性能预测模型；3) 通过共形预测（conformal prediction）校准预测不确定性。

Result: 在自动驾驶和四足速度跟踪任务上验证：sim-to-sim研究中，SCAPE相对于场景条件化神经网络和聚合统计基线将场景级预测误差降低4.9%/34.7%（驾驶）和14.5%/27.7%（四足）。在实体Unitree Go2上部署的速度跟踪策略评估中，SCAPE提高了测试样本效率，产生更窄的校准预测区间，更好地泛化到分布外场景，并支持细粒度部署策略。

Conclusion: SCAPE通过场景条件化的预测和不确定性校准，为策略部署提供了更可靠、更精细的评估工具，显著优于聚合级别的基线方法，并支持在实际场景中的安全部署决策。

Abstract: Reliable performance evaluation is a central bottleneck for deploying robot-learning policies in real-world conditions. Real-world testing is faithful but costly and difficult to scale, whereas simulation-based testing scales easily but is inevitably biased by the sim-to-real gap. Existing simulation-augmented methods combine limited real-world rollouts with abundant simulation proxies, but focus on performance averaged over initial conditions and deployment settings. Such population-level averages obscure scenario-specific variation and provide limited guidance about when and where a policy can be safely deployed. We propose SCAPE, a scenario-conditioned simulation-augmented policy evaluation framework that predicts scenario-conditioned real-world policy performance using limited paired sim-and-real samples and large-scale simulation rollouts. SCAPE corrects sim-to-real bias in simulation labels before training the prediction model and calibrates prediction uncertainty through conformal prediction. We validate SCAPE on autonomous driving and quadruped velocity tracking. In sim-to-sim studies, SCAPE reduces scenario-level prediction error by 4.9%/34.7% (driving) and 14.5%/27.7% (quadruped) relative to scene-conditioned neural and aggregate statistical baselines on average. We further evaluate a velocity-tracking policy deployed on a physical Unitree Go2. SCAPE also improves testing sample efficiency, produces narrower calibrated prediction intervals, generalizes better to out-of-distribution scenarios, and enables fine-grained deployment strategies.

</details>


### [133] [Hybrid Feedback Sampling for Sample-Efficient Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19443)
*Chaoyi Pan,Zeji Yi,John Zhang,Zachary Manchester,Guannan Qu,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出反馈采样MPC（FS-MPC）算法，解决采样型MPC在高维、开环不稳定动态系统中样本效率差和数值不稳定的问题。通过混合采样设计（结合局部与全局搜索）实现最优采样提议分布，理论证明其收敛速度快于标准MPPI且最优性优于纯反馈采样，并在人类形机器人操作等任务及真实世界中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样型MPC虽具有并行性和灵活性优势，但对高维、开环不稳定的动态系统，所需样本量随规划时域指数增长，导致样本效率低下和数值不稳定，现有方法（如MPPI、纯反馈采样）难以兼顾收敛速度与最优性。

Method: 提出FS-MPC算法。核心思想是证明最优采样提议分布可通过使用优化后的反馈策略进行采样来实现。算法包含混合采样设计，根据系统稳定性与可用计算预算在局部搜索与全局搜索之间进行平衡。

Result: 理论分析表明混合采样方法比标准MPPI收敛更快，比标准反馈采样最优性更好。在多种接触丰富控制任务（如人形机器人的操控与灵巧手操作）中，FS-MPC能成功处理标准采样方法难以应对的动态不稳定任务，并严格优于纯反馈策略。最终在真实世界的人形机器人行走与操控任务中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: FS-MPC通过混合采样策略有效解决采样型MPC在开环不稳定系统中的样本效率与数值稳定性问题，在理论和实验层面均验证了其优越性，为接触丰富、动态不稳定的机器人控制提供了实用且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Thanks to its parallelizability and flexibility, sampling-based Model Predictive Control (MPC) has become widely popular for controlling real-world robotic systems. However, for high-dimensional and open-loop unstable dynamical systems, the required number of samples to improve the control sequence will grow exponentially with the horizon, leading to poor sample efficiency and numerical instability. This paper investigates the instability of shooting methods in sampling-based MPC and shows that the optimal sampling proposal distribution can be realized by sampling with an optimized feedback policy. We refer to this algorithm as Feedback Sampling MPC (FS-MPC). FS-MPC involves a hybrid sampling design which balances local and global search based on the system stability and the available computation budget. Our theoretical analysis shows that our hybrid sampling approach achieves faster convergence than standard MPPI and better optimality than standard feedback sampling. Empirically, in diverse contact-rich control tasks like humanoid loco-manipulation and dexterous manipulation, we show that FS-MPC successfully tackles dynamically unstable tasks where standard sample-based approaches struggle, and strictly outperforms feedback policies alone. Finally, we validate our method on humanoid robot locomotion and manipulation tasks in the real world.

</details>


### [134] [When Automata Meet Streams: Temporal Logic Compilation for Stream-Based Robotics Task and Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19453)
*Sayem Nazmuz Zaman,Cyrus Neary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SAM-TD方法，首次将任意LTL_f时序约束编译到基于流的机器人任务与运动规划（TAMP）求解器中，通过自动机守卫嵌入动作模式和同步状态更新，支持规划过程中动态生成的流对象，并在三个PDDLStream环境和标准PDDL基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于流的TAMP规划器通常只考虑目标可达性，而长时程任务还需要满足时序规范（如安全关键排序、不变性、活性约束）。然而流的迭代精化过程会动态生成不断扩展的几何对象集合，使得现有时序逻辑编译方法无法直接适用，目前没有任何方法能在流式求解器中强制此类时序约束。

Method: SAM-TD将任意LTL_f约束翻译为自动机，并将回归后的自动机守卫嵌入动作模式中（在规划开始前预定义）；在搜索过程中同步更新自动机状态，并通过所有自动机共享的有效性令牌来剪枝违反约束的搜索分支。该方法无需枚举固定对象集或修改底层规划器，天然支持流在规划过程中动态生成的对象。

Result: 实验首次展示在三个机器人PDDLStream环境中实现流式TAMP在LTL_f约束下的求解，同时在标准离散PDDL基准上与最先进的时序约束编译方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: SAM-TD成功弥合了流式TAMP与形式化时序约束之间的鸿沟，通过编译方法在不修改规划器的情况下将LTL_f约束无缝集成到流式求解流程中，为长时程机器人任务规划提供了新的解决方案基础。

Abstract: Stream-based robotics Task and Motion Planning (TAMP) integrates discrete symbolic planning with dynamically generated continuous geometric parameters, such as poses, grasps, and trajectories. However, stream-based planners typically reason only about goal reachability, whereas long-horizon tasks also demand adherence to temporal specifications, such as safety-critical ordering, invariance, and liveness constraints. No methods currently exist to enforce such temporal constraints for stream-based solvers because streams generate an expanding geometric object set via iterative stream refinement loops during planning, rendering existing temporal-logic compilation techniques incompatible. We therefore present Synchronous Action Monitoring with Token Destruction (SAM-TD), a compilation method that enforces arbitrary Linear Temporal Logic over finite traces ($\textrm{LTL}_f$) specifications in stream-based TAMP. SAM-TD translates arbitrary $\textrm{LTL}_f$ constraints into automata and embeds regressed automaton guards into action schemas, which are pre-specified before planning begins. By doing so, SAM-TD can handle objects generated by streams during planning, thus circumventing the need to enumerate a fixed object set or modify the underlying planner. During search, SAM-TD synchronously updates automaton states and uses a validity token shared across all automata to prune constraint-violating branches. We show that SAM-TD supports dynamically generated stream objects from iterative stream refinements during plan search. Experimental results provide the first ever demonstration of stream-based TAMP under $\textrm{LTL}_f$ constraints in three robotics PDDLStream environments. Furthermore, on standard discrete PDDL benchmarks, SAM-TD is competitive with state-of-the-art temporal-constraint compilation methods.

</details>


### [135] [Fine-Tuning VLAs with Self-Demonstrated Generative Control for Multi-Task Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19490)
*Prachi Garg,Steve Xing,Prahit Yaugand,Saurabh Gupta,Derek Hoiem*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: State-of-the-art vision-language-action (VLA) models such as $π_{0.5}$ exhibit strong semantic understanding, instruction following and task behavior. However, when deployed on new robots, even minor mismatches in hardware configuration relative to pretraining can cause severe performance drops. Finetuning the VLA on in-domain expert data from the new embodiment improves performance on the expert task but leads to a loss in its original instruction following and behavioral priors. In this paper, we propose a self-supervised method that generates online interaction rollouts from the zero-shot VLA as additional training data for finetuning. Our experiments show this finetuning scheme yields strong multi-task policies that, on the target robot, (1) inherit prior tasks distilled from the zero-shot model, (2) enable generalist instruction following, while (3) learning new skills from expert data with improved sample efficiency. We demonstrate the success of our approach across test sets probing generalization on a real ALOHA robot and a new simulation benchmark in RoboTwin. Video results are available at https://self-supervised-control.pages.dev/

</details>


### [136] [LF-GICP: Parameter-Free Degeneracy-Aware LiDAR Odometry via a Voxel-Normal Localizability Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19522)
*Eunsoo Im*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需参数调节的退化环境LiDAR里程计方法LF-GICP。作者指出在体素化GICP中GN Hessian会掩盖平移退化（因协方差正则化使平移块病态良置），因此改用无正则化的体素法向局部性场及其两个统计量（各向异性分数f0和信息缺失质量λ0），结合时间中值门控触发Fisher信息对应加权。该方法在两个短序列上由固定规则校准一次后冻结，在KITTI上取得最低相对平移误差0.865%，并在GEODE隧道、MulRan、HeLiPR及四种传感器上均优于基线且无需重调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扫描到地图的LiDAR里程计在几何退化环境（如隧道、走廊）中会沿不可观测轴产生无界漂移，而现有退化处理方法需要针对不同环境进行参数调节，缺乏通用性。

Method: 1) 证明体素化GICP中GN Hessian掩盖了平移方向的退化，原因在于协方差正则化使平移块保持人为良好条件数；2) 提出无正则化的体素法向局部性场，并提取两个统计量：归一化分数f0检测方向各向异性，绝对逐体素质量λ0区分信息缺失（隧道）与信息稀释（开阔场景）；3) 用时间中值门控融合两者，触发Fisher信息对应加权；4) 仅通过固定规则在两个短序列上标定一次后冻结参数。

Result: 在相同的评估协议下，LF-GICP在KITTI上取得最低相对平移误差0.865%，优于重新运行的基线；在GEODE隧道和MulRan上超越基线；在HeLiPR均值上领先；并在四种传感器类型上无需重调即可泛化。此外，实验证明直而均匀的隧道在LiDAR-only配准下沿其轴向本质上不可观测。

Conclusion: LF-GICP通过识别并绕过GICP的Hessian退化掩盖问题，以参数无关的方式实现了退化环境中的稳健里程计，在多种数据集和传感器上展现出优异的泛化能力；同时明确了纯LiDAR配准在特定退化场景下的理论极限。

Abstract: Scan-to-map LiDAR odometry drifts unboundedly along the unobservable axes of geometrically degenerate environments like tunnels and corridors, and existing degeneracy handling requires environment-specific parameter tuning. This paper presents a parameter-free approach. We show that in voxelized GICP the Gauss--Newton (GN) Hessian masks translational degeneracy, because covariance regularization keeps the translation block artificially well-conditioned. We bypass this with a regularization-free voxel-normal localizability field and two of its statistics: a normalized fraction $f_0$ detecting directional anisotropy, and an absolute per-voxel mass $λ_0$ distinguishing information absence (tunnels) from dilution (dense open scenes). A temporal-median gate combines both to trigger Fisher-information correspondence weighting. Calibrated once by fixed rules on two short sequences and then frozen, LF-GICP achieves the lowest KITTI relative translation error ($0.865\%$) under an identical evaluation protocol against re-run baselines, outperforms them on GEODE tunnels and MulRan, leads the HeLiPR mean, and generalizes across four sensor types without re-tuning. We further demonstrate empirically that straight, uniform tunnels remain unobservable along their axis for LiDAR-only registration.

</details>


### [137] [Multimodal Trajectory Planning for Surface Vehicles using Turning Circle-based Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19537)
*Changyu Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向自主水面航行器在动态环境中的无引导路径多模态轨迹规划框架，通过将模型预测控制（MPC）与基于转弯圆的控制屏障函数（TC-CBF）相结合，生成左转和右转两种不同的避碰模式，使优化求解器能够探索拓扑上不同的轨迹，从而缓解单模态MPC的局部极小值和死锁问题，同时保持计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于欧氏距离的控制屏障函数（ED-CBF）仅依据距离邻近性评估安全性，忽略了水面航行器的非完整运动学和有限转弯能力，无法准确刻画避让可行域。此外，许多多模态轨迹规划方法依赖于全局规划的引导路径，在动态环境下灵活性受限，且单模态MPC易陷入局部极小值和死锁问题。

Method: 将MPC与基于转弯圆的控制屏障函数（TC-CBF）集成。TC-CBF利用航行器转弯圆的几何构型确定可行的避让区域，生成左转和右转两种不同的避让模式，并将避让方向直接嵌入安全约束中，使优化求解器无需全局引导路径即可探索拓扑上不同的轨迹。

Result: 在包含多个运动船舶的广泛仿真实验中，相比单模态基线方法，所提方法在所有测试交通密度下均实现了更高的成功率、更少的安全违例次数以及更小的残余违例量。

Conclusion: 该框架通过将避让方向直接编码进安全约束，实现了无需引导路径的多模态轨迹规划，在缓解局部极小值和死锁问题的同时保持了计算效率，为动态水域中的自主水面航行器避碰提供了有效方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a guide path-free multimodal trajectory planning framework for autonomous surface vehicles operating in dynamic environments. The proposed method integrates model predictive control (MPC) with a turning circle-based control barrier function (TC-CBF). Unlike conventional Euclidean distance-based CBFs (ED-CBFs), which evaluate safety solely based on proximity, the TC-CBF accounts for the nonholonomic motion and finite turning capability of a surface vehicle. Its geometric formulation identifies feasible avoidance regions according to the vehicle's turning circles and generates distinct left- and right-turning avoidance modes. These modes allow the optimization solver to explore and select topologically different trajectories without relying on globally planned guide paths, as required by many conventional multimodal planning approaches. By embedding the avoidance direction directly into the safety constraint, the proposed framework alleviates the local-minimum and deadlock problems of single-mode MPC while maintaining computational efficiency. Extensive simulations involving multiple moving vessels demonstrate that the proposed method achieves higher success rates, fewer safety violations, and smaller residual violations than single-mode baselines across all tested traffic densities.

</details>


### [138] [HiTac-WAM: A Hierarchical Tactile World Action Model for Contact-Rich Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19574)
*Chao Xue,Chaofan Zhang,Wenxuan Ma,Guocai Yao,Shaowei Cui,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiTac-WAM是一个分层触觉世界动作模型，将未来触觉状态预测分解为接触状态、3D变形场和滑移风险三个层级，利用有向注意力掩码和停止梯度信号组织层级间的物理依赖关系，并在规划与执行中利用触觉预测进行候选动作排序和错误触发重规划，显著提升了真实机器人操作的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型虽能联合预测未来视觉观测和动作，但触觉感知变体通常将未来触觉表示为图像或潜在流，未建模触觉状态之间物理依赖的分层组织结构（如接触→变形→滑移的因果链），导致触觉预测缺乏结构化物理约束。

Method: HiTac-WAM将未来触觉预测分解为接触状态、3D变形场和滑移风险三层，构成有向层级；每个下游阶段依赖前序阶段的停止梯度信号。通过有向注意力掩码，使触觉查询可关注每个候选动作的视频-动作上下文，同时阻止视频和动作查询关注触觉标记。规划阶段基于触觉预测和任务进度估计对候选动作块排序；执行阶段保留选定的触觉预测作为参考，预测与观测间的持续偏差触发纠正性重规划。

Result: HiTac-WAM达到平均接触F1为0.921；在相同训练预算下，有向层级使3D位移L2误差相对仅变形的预测器降低17.6%，滑移AUPRC相对仅滑移的预测器提升60.4%。在芯片抓取、黑板擦除和USB插入任务中，层级预测引导的选择将平均真实机器人成功率从31.1%提升到61.1%，完整系统达到72.2%。

Conclusion: 对触觉状态进行有向层级分解（接触→变形→滑移）能够更准确地捕获触觉世界的物理依赖关系，其结构化预测不仅能优化候选动作的选择，还通过可预测-观测偏差检测触发重规划，从而显著提升真实机器人操作性能。

Abstract: World action models jointly predict future visual observations and actions, whereas existing tactile-aware variants typically represent future touch as an image or latent stream without modeling the physical dependencies that organize tactile states hierarchically. We present HiTac-WAM, a hierarchical tactile world action model that forecasts a sequence of future tactile states for each candidate action chunk before execution. The forecast factorizes into contact state, a 3D deformation field, and slip risk, organized as a directed hierarchy in which each downstream stage is conditioned on stop-gradient signals from preceding stages. A directed attention mask allows tactile queries to attend to the video-action context of each candidate while preventing video and action queries from attending to tactile tokens. For planning, HiTac-WAM ranks candidate action chunks using tactile forecasts and task-progress estimates. For execution, the selected tactile forecast is retained as a reference; persistent discrepancies between predicted and observed tactile states trigger corrective replanning. HiTac-WAM achieves a mean contact F1 of 0.921; under matched training budgets, the directed hierarchy reduces 3D displacement L2 error by 17.6% relative to the deformation-only predictor and improves slip AUPRC by 60.4% relative to the slip-only predictor. Across chip grasping, blackboard erasing, and USB insertion, selection guided by the hierarchical forecasts increases the average real-robot success rate from 31.1% to 61.1%, while the full system attains 72.2%.

</details>


### [139] [OrthoSkillVLA: Continual Skill Learning via Gradient-Informed Skill Subspace Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19589)
*Jiaqi Wang,Zhou Fang,Qiongfeng Shi,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出OrthoSkillVLA，一个针对预训练视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的参数高效持续技能学习框架。它分析VLA内部组件（VLM和ActionHead）的异质性表征，对二者施加分离的子空间约束以分别保护语义表征与局部速度模式；同时引入轻量级特征感知MoE解码器，通过无需训练的router按特征空间亲和度选择专家，解决了输出层冻结导致表达瓶颈或更新导致旧技能覆盖的问题，在无演示回放条件下提升了持续技能学习的保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练VLA模型顺序适配多样技能时会产生灾难性遗忘。现有方法存在不足：架构隔离方法提高保留率但增大推理开销；子空间约束方法限制参数更新以最小化干扰，但对整个模型施加统一约束，忽略了VLA内部组件的异质性（VLM维护广泛语义表征易容量耗尽，ActionHead将语义细化为局部速度模式且对扰动高度敏感）。此外，最终速度解码器作为读出层，冻结会形成输出端表达瓶颈，更新则可能覆盖旧速度映射。

Method: OrthoSkillVLA包含两部分核心设计：(1) 针对VLM和ActionHead的异质性，施加分离的正交子空间约束——VLM在正交子空间内更新以保留可复用的语义容量，ActionHead同样受限以保护局部速度模式；(2) 提出特征感知MoE解码器替代传统输出层，每个技能分配一个紧凑专家，并采用无需训练的router根据特征空间亲和度选择对应专家，实现参数高效且无演示回放的持续技能学习。

Result: 在大量仿真和真实世界实验以及消融研究中，OrthoSkillVLA在获取新技能的同时更好地保持了先前技能，优于现有持续学习方法。

Conclusion: 通过识别VLA组件的异质性角色并针对性地设计分离约束与特征感知MoE解码器，OrthoSkillVLA在无演示回放的条件下实现了VLA模型的高效持续技能学习，平衡了旧技能保留与新技能获取。

Abstract: Pretrained Vision-Language-Action models provide a strong foundation for robot learning, but sequentially adapting them to diverse skills can perturb the representations and velocity mappings used by previous skills, leading to catastrophic forgetting. Architecture-based approaches improve retention by isolating skills but lead to increased inference footprint. Recent subspace-constrained methods restrict parameter updates in an orthogonal subspace to minimize interference but impose a unified constraint on the entire model. We analyze the distinct roles of internal VLA components and identify two VLA-specific challenges. First, the VLM maintains broad semantic representations, making it vulnerable to capacity exhaustion, whereas the ActionHead refines semantics into localized velocity patterns that are highly sensitive to perturbations. Second, the final velocity decoder serves as a readout layer. Freezing it forms an output-stage expressivity bottleneck, while updating it risks overwriting previous velocity mappings. To this end, we propose OrthoSkillVLA, a parameter-efficient framework for continual skill learning in pretrained VLA models without demonstration replay. Given the representation heterogeneity, we impose separate subspace constraints on the VLM and ActionHead, preserving reusable semantic capacity while protecting localized velocity patterns. For the output layer, we introduce a lightweight feature-aware MoE decoder, where each skill is allocated a compact expert and a training-free router selects the expert according to feature-space affinity. Extensive simulated and real-world evaluations, together with ablations, demonstrate that OrthoSkillVLA better preserves prior skills while acquiring new ones.

</details>


### [140] [The Verification Gap in Networked Physical AI: A Post-Semantic Communication Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19593)
*Shunsuke Saruwatari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出后语义通信框架（Post-Semantic Communication Framework），用于解决网络化物理AI中"提议有效但证据/授权不足"的验证鸿沟问题。框架涵盖证据要求声明、证据记录表示、证据充分性与授权定案分离、运行时门控，并区分证据传输与证据协调两种机制。受控通信研究表明，发送方定案与接收方定案的反馈存在不对称性。最后提出情景级报告模式以统一未来物理AI研究度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在物理AI网络中，一个任务有效的提议（proposal）即使语义正确且及时，也可能因缺乏有效的证据或定案所需的授权而无法形成合理的物理动作。这种"提议形成"与"物理执行"之间的不匹配（称为验证鸿沟，verification gap）是现有通信框架未充分处理的系统接口问题。

Method: 1) 以应用声明的证据需求为起点构建框架；2) 将合格观测表示为证据记录（evidence records）；3) 通过单一路径验证支持性与冲突性记录；4) 将证据充分性判断与授权定案分离，并设置下游运行时门控；5) 区分证据传输（扩大定案端可达的记录集）与证据协调（抑制定案端已持有记录的冗余传输）；6) 用有限状态机框架检查验证评估器是否一致实现声明的区分；7) 设计情景级报告模式提供统一实验基准。

Result: 受控通信实验揭示了定案者依赖的不对称性：发送方定案（sender-finalized）的Feedback利用证据传输扩展了整个可行绘图区域内的证据可达性；而接收方定案（receiver-finalized）的Feedback利用证据协调抑制冗余载荷，直到丢包、延迟、新鲜度与截止期成本使选择转向One-way方案。有限状态机检查确认评估器一致实现了所声明的框架区分。

Conclusion: 验证鸿沟是物理AI系统中不可忽略的接口问题，后语义通信框架通过区分证据充分性与授权定案、并解耦证据传输与证据协调，提供了一套可验证的系统化解决方案。情景级报告模式为未来物理AI通信研究提供了可比的度量标准。

Abstract: A task-effective proposal is not yet a justified physical action. In networked Physical AI, a proposal may be understood while valid, timely, proposal-bound evidence or the authority required to finalize an action remains unavailable. We call this mismatch the verification gap and introduce a Post-Semantic Communication Framework for the systems interface between proposal formation and physical execution. The framework begins with application-declared evidence requirements, represents qualifying observations as evidence records, validates supporting and conflicting records through one path, and separates evidence sufficiency from authorized finalization and a downstream runtime gate. It further distinguishes evidence transfer, which can enlarge the record set reachable by a finalizer, from evidence coordination, which can suppress transmission around records already held at the finalization endpoint. Finite-state framework checks verify that the evaluator implements the declared distinctions consistently. Under the declared model, the controlled communication study exposes a finalizer-dependent asymmetry: sender-finalized Feedback uses evidence transfer to expand evidence reachability throughout the feasible plotted region, whereas receiver-finalized Feedback uses coordination to suppress redundant payload until loss, latency, freshness, and deadline costs shift selection to One-way. Finally, an episode-level reporting schema defines common denominators for future measured Physical-AI studies.

</details>


### [141] [What Matters for Latent Actions in Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19613)
*Xizhou Bu,Qingda Hu,Lei Zhou,Lingfeng Zhang,Yingbo Tang,Zihao Liu,Xinyi Tao,Zhiqiang Ma,Qingqiu Huang,Chufeng Tang,Hongbo Wang,Jing Zhang,Jiayi Ma,Hangjun Ye,Wei Li,Xiaoshuai Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次对隐式动作建模（LAM）进行系统性的实证研究，在统一的自动编码框架下探索了41种LAM设计选择（涵盖建模范式、学习目标与正则化、动作整合策略三个维度），并评估了四种代理指标对下游机器人操作性能的预测能力，最终通过三个基准和真实世界任务验证了微调VLM主干网络作为LAM初始化的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LAM是利用大规模无标签视频学习隐式动作（作为物理动作的紧凑替代）以赋能机器人学习的范式，但该领域研究高度碎片化：各方法在互不一致的实验条件下孤立评估不同设计选择，难以辨识真正决定下游操作性能的关键因素。

Method: 将代表性LAM方法统一整合到公共自动编码框架中，系统性地从三个维度考察41种LAM设计选择（隐式动作建模范式、学习目标与正则化方法、隐式动作整合策略）；同时建立四种代理指标以评估隐式动作质量，并验证其是否能可靠预测下游机器人操作性能。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的基准测试上进行的大量实验提供了强经验证据：使用隐式动作微调视觉语言模型（VLM）主干网络，能为下游策略学习提供更强的初始化；该结论在真实世界机器人操作任务中得到了进一步验证。

Conclusion: 微调VLM主干网络作为LAM训练范式是与众不同的设计选择，可显著提升下游任务表现；所提出的四种代理指标在预测下游操作性能方面具有一定的可靠性，为LAM领域提供了统一测评框架和设计指南。

Abstract: Latent Action Models (LAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for enabling robot learning to leverage large-scale unlabeled videos through latent actions that serve as compact surrogates for physical actions. Despite rapid progress, research on LAM remains highly fragmented, with existing methods evaluating different design choices in isolation under inconsistent experimental settings, making it difficult to identify the factors that truly determine downstream robotic manipulation performance.
