In this work, we present the first comprehensive empirical study of latent action learning for robotic manipulation. We unify representative LAM methods within a common autoencoding framework and systematically investigate 41 LAM design choices across three dimensions, including latent action modeling paradigms, learning objectives and regularization methods, and latent action integration strategies. We further examine four proxy metrics for evaluating latent action quality and assess their ability to reliably predict downstream robotic manipulation performance. Extensive experiments on three widely used benchmarks provide strong empirical evidence that fine-tuning vision-language model (VLM) backbones with latent actions provides a stronger initialization for downstream policy learning, with further validation on real-world robot manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [142] [Magnetically Self-Sealed MR Haptic Actuator With PWM-Based Excitation and High-Fidelity Torque Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19635)
*Dong Qiang,Tian Yuan,Song Yang,Kequan Xia,Thomas Reddyhoff,Yikun Zhang,Cheng Cheng,Min Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成磁流变液（MRF）触觉系统，包含紧凑型磁自密封旋转执行器、低迟滞PWM驱动、高保真模型化扭矩渲染以及长时间稳定运行能力。通过磁静力学仿真优化磁场分布，实现最大600 N·mm/A输出。实验表明10 kHz PWM显著降低迟滞，所提出的混合控制器（前馈+迟滞补偿+PI反馈+滑模修正）相比PID分别降低了77.4%、61.9%和68.3%的方波过冲、下冲和稳态RMSE，且1.5小时运行仅温升2.5°C。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRF执行器在触觉反馈中具有紧凑和快速可控的优势，但实际应用面临流体密封、低迟滞激励、精确扭矩控制和长期稳定运行四大挑战。现有方案难以同时满足这些要求，需要一种集成化的系统解决方案来提升人机交互的安全性和感知质量。

Method: 采用集成化设计：（1）磁静力学仿真指导磁/非磁材料排布，在多盘扭矩区和永磁密封区聚焦磁通，实现磁自密封的紧凑旋转执行器；（2）研究不同PWM频率对迟滞和重复性的影响，选择10 kHz并构建随扭矩变化方向与速度变化的非线性模型；（3）设计结合前馈、迟滞补偿、PI反馈和滑模修正的实时控制器，与PID进行对比评估。

Result: 最大输出达600 N·mm/A；10 kHz PWM下迟滞显著降低；混合控制器相比PID在方波过冲、下冲和稳态RMSE上分别降低77.4%、61.9%和68.3%；能够跟踪正弦及生物力学模型参考信号；1.5小时连续运行仅线圈附近温升2.5°C，无明显的跟踪性能损失。

Conclusion: 该高保真扭矩渲染系统有效解决了MRF触觉执行器的密封、迟滞、控制和稳定性问题，将从根本上推动人机协作向更安全、更高效、更直观的方向发展。

Abstract: Accurate and stable torque rendering is essential for safe and perceptive human--machine interaction. Magnetorheological fluid (MRF)-based actuators offer a compact and rapidly controllable solution for haptic feedback, but their practical implementation requires reliable fluid sealing, low-hysteresis excitation, accurate torque control, and stable long-duration operation. This article presents an integrated MRF haptic system featuring a compact magnetically self-sealed rotary actuator, low-hysteresis PWM operation, high-fidelity model-based torque rendering, and stable performance during long-time operation. Magnetostatic simulation guides the arrangement of magnetic and nonmagnetic materials to focus flux in the multidisk torque and permanent-magnet sealing regions, enabling a maximum 600 N$\cdot$mm/A output. Experiments show that higher PWM frequencies reduce hysteresis and improve repeatability. At 10 kHz, the response is represented by a nonlinear model that varies with the direction and speed of torque change. The real-time controller combines feedforward, hysteresis compensation, PI feedback, and sliding-mode correction. Compared with PID, it reduces square-wave overshoot, undershoot, and steady-state RMSE by 77.4\%, 61.9\%, and 68.3\%, respectively. It tracks sinusoidal and biomechanics-model-based references, and a 1.5-h test shows only a 2.5 $^\circ$C rise near the coil with no clear tracking loss. This high-fidelity torque rendering will fundamentally transform human--robot collaboration by making interactions safer, more efficient, and more intuitive.

</details>


### [143] [World-Model-Grounded LLM Planning for AUV and ASV Navigation Near Offshore Wind Farms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19661)
*Markus Buchholz,Ignacio Carlucho,Yvan R. Petillot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用物理世界模型增强LLM规划器，使其具备物理感知能力（如指令运行时长、避障判断），包含物理神经世界模型、三阶段梯度轨迹优化器和带信任域的MPC闭环重规划器。在6-DOF AUV和3-DOF ASV上验证，零碰撞到达所有目标，较无物理基线的GazeboSim目标距离误差降低70-93%，并引入VLM语义映射管线替代人工障碍物几何标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）能将自然语言任务转化为机器人动作序列，但缺乏物理世界感知：无法判断指令应执行多久，也无法预判动作是否会导致机器人漂移碰撞障碍物。这一短板限制了LLM在真实物理环境中部署的可靠性与安全性。

Method: 提出三组件框架：1）物理约束的神经世界模型，为LLM提供动力学仿真基础；2）三阶段基于梯度的轨迹优化器，生成可行物理轨迹；3）带信任域保护的MPC式闭环重规划器，确保实时纠偏。LLM负责决策"做什么"，世界模型负责决定"做多久"，适用于6-DOF（AUV）与3-DOF（ASV）等不同车辆构型。另为ASV设计了基于VLM的语义映射管线，从卫星图像、海图和API预报中提取障碍物与环境上下文。

Result: 在5个基准任务中，两种平台均零预测碰撞到达所有目标；在GazeboSim含洋流、波浪与推进器动力学的仿真中保持无碰撞，目标距离误差较无grounded基线降低70-82%（ASV）和约93%（AUV）。残差微调进一步将代理rollout的RMSE降低60%（AUV）和69%（ASV）。VLM语义映射管线达到96%的可通航性准确率，可作为手工标注障碍物几何的替代方案。

Conclusion: 世界模型能有效弥补LLM规划器在物理推理上的缺陷，将高层语义决策与低层物理可行性解耦，显著提升轨迹安全性与跟踪精度。VLM辅助的语义映射进一步消除了对外部传感器与人工标注的依赖，为无GPS/无传感器环境下的部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large language models can turn a natural-language mission into a sequence of robot actions, but they do not have a sense of physics: they cannot judge how long a command should run, or whether it will make the robot drift into an obstacle. We proposed the use of a world model to expand the capabilities of Large Language model-based planners. Our method has three components: a physics-grounded neural world model, a three-phase gradient-based trajectory optimizer, and a Model Predictive Controller (MPC)-style closed-loop replanner with a trust-region guard. The language model decides what to do, and the world model decides how long, whether that means driving eight thrusters through 6 DOF or two differential thrusters through 3 DOF. We evaluate two marine vehicle classes operating near offshore wind infrastructure: a 6-DOF Autonomous Underwater Vehicle (AUV) and a 3-DOF differential-drive Autonomous Surface Vehicle (ASV). In five benchmark missions per platform, both vehicles reach every goal with zero predicted collisions, and both transfer to GazeboSim under ocean current, waves, and thruster dynamics, remaining collision-free and cutting GazeboSim goal-distance error versus the ungrounded baseline by 70-82% (ASV) and roughly 93% (AUV), after a residual fine-tuning pass that separately reduces surrogate rollout Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) by 60% (AUV) and 69% (ASV). For the ASV we further demonstrate a Vision language model (VLM)-assisted semantic-mapping pipeline that extracts obstacles and environmental context from satellite imagery, nautical charts, and forecast Application Programming Interface (API) instead of onboard sensors, reaching 96% navigability accuracy as a drop-in replacement for hand-specified obstacle geometry.

</details>


### [144] [SAGE: Ergodic Control for Autonomous and Adaptive Inspection of Subsea Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19671)
*Markus Buchholz,Ignacio Carlucho,Yvan R. Petillot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE（语义与自适应生成遍历性）架构，一种基于遍历控制的水下自主机器人巡检方法，通过实时传感器推导的风险分布动态分配巡检时间，而非预设航线。在双水下采油树（五阀门）仿真中，SAGE对高风险阀门的重访间隔为5.8秒，优于固定A*路线的8.1秒，且能在新泄漏被发现后自动调整行为，无需重新规划或操作员介入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下采油树阀门需要持续巡检，但不同阀门在同一时刻的风险并不相同：疑似泄漏的阀门需要比历史清洁的阀门更频繁地被检查，且随着任务推进，新发现的泄漏会动态改变风险图景。传统的固定路线（如A*路径）无法根据实时风险分布自适应调整巡检频率，需要一种按风险比例分配机器人时间的控制架构。

Method: 作者提出SAGE（Semantic and Adaptive Generative Ergodicity）架构，利用遍历控制（ergodic control）思想，将巡检时间与实时传感器推导出的风险分布成正比进行分配。控制律持续重算而非一次性规划，使新检测到的泄漏能在下一个控制周期内自动改变机器人行为，无需显式重规划步骤。在双水下采油树场景（共5个阀门）中进行仿真，并与固定循环A*路径进行对比。

Result: 仿真结果表明，两种方法都可以调到在高风险阀门附近花费相似的总体时间，但只有遍历控制能更频繁地检查该阀门：在优势风险阀门上，遍历控制的平均重访间隔为5.8秒，而A*对所有阀门（无论风险大小）都固定为8.1秒。这意味着泄漏可能被漏检的时间在遍历控制下仅为固定路线的约三分之二。

Conclusion: 遍历控制架构SAGE在动态风险场景下显著优于固定路线规划，能够基于实时传感器信息自适应调整巡检行为，缩短高风险管理的关键重访间隔，且在无需操作员介入和显式重规划的情况下即可响应新检测到的泄漏。

Abstract: Subsea Christmas Trees (XTs) are underwater structures that use valves for directing oil flow, needing constant inspection. But not every valve carries the same risk at the same time: a valve with a suspected leak needs to be revisited far more often than one with a clean history, and that risk picture changes during the mission as new leaks are found. To handle this, we present SAGE (Semantic and Adaptive Generative Ergodicity), an ergodic-control architecture that allocates vehicle time in proportion to a live, sensor-derived risk distribution rather than a scripted route. We study a two-XT scenario, with five valves in total, and compare a fixed-loop A* tour against SAGE. Both methods can be tuned to spend similar total time near a high-risk valve, but only ergodic control also checks it more often: in simulation, a dominant-risk valve was revisited every 5.8 s under ergodic control against a fixed 8.1 s for every valve under A*, regardless of risk, so a leak can go unnoticed for barely two-thirds as long. Because the tracked distribution is recomputed rather than planned once, a newly detected leak shifts vehicle behavior on the next control cycle with no explicit re-planning step and no operator in the loop, which a fixed tour cannot do without a discrete re-route. We derive the ergodic control law behind this behavior and report simulation results on the five-valve scenario.

</details>


### [145] [Keeping the Franka Emika Panda alive: a ROS 2 stack with a reliable position interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19740)
*Antonio Langella,Davide Risi,Vincenzo Petrone,Enrico Ferrentino,Pasquale Chiacchio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个开源软件栈，恢复了Franka Emika Panda机器人在ROS 2中的支持，并解决了其外部位置控制接口长期以来的不可靠问题。通过分析发现振动和防护停止的根源在于外部控制回路的时序和采样抖动，而非机器人本身限制，并提出了异步硬件接口、速率匹配机制和位置域参考生成策略，实验验证了该架构的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Franka Emika Panda机器人的外部位置控制接口长期存在不可靠问题，包括振动和意外防护停止，这严重阻碍了基于ROS 2的现代机器人应用开发。现有官方实现存在根本缺陷，导致无法安全、稳定地进行精确控制，因此有必要深入分析其根因并提出解决方案。

Method: 首先分析外部位置控制不稳定的根本原因，证实振动源于外部控制回路的时序与采样抖动。在此基础上提出三部分解决方案：(1)异步硬件接口，将实时通信与ROS 2控制循环解耦；(2)用于较慢指令源的速率匹配机制；(3)位置域参考生成策略，生成可靠平滑的位置指令。

Result: 实验验证表明，所提出的架构通过减少官方实现引入的运动伪影，可以可靠地跟踪速度参考。该软件栈已在两个独立的Panda平台上，跨运动规划、柔顺控制、位置控制操作和触觉遥操作等多项任务中验证通过。

Conclusion: 通过恢复Panda机器人现代化、可靠且开放ROS 2的生态系统，该工作降低了开发安全、响应迅速、可复现的人-机器人协作应用的准入门槛，促进了规划、感知、交互和共享自主的集成。

Abstract: This paper presents an open-source software stack that restores ROS 2 support for the Franka Emika Panda robot while resolving the long-standing unreliability of its external position control interface. We first analyze the root causes of unstable position control and show that the observed vibrations and protective stops arise from the timing of the external control loop and sampling jitter, rather than from limitations of the robot itself. Building on this analysis, we introduce an asynchronous hardware interface that decouples real-time communication from the ROS 2 control loop, a rate-matching mechanism for slower command sources, and a position-domain reference generation strategy that produces reliable, smooth position commands. Experimental validation shows that the proposed architecture reliably tracks velocity references by reducing motion artifacts introduced by the official implementation, and the stack is validated across motion planning, compliance control, position-controlled manipulation, and haptic teleoperation on two independent Panda platforms. By restoring a modern, reliable, and open ROS 2 ecosystem for the Panda, this work lowers the barrier to developing safe, responsive, and reproducible human-robot collaboration applications that integrate planning, perception, interaction, and shared autonomy. Code and videos are available on our website at https://sites.google.com/view/fer-ros2/.

</details>


### [146] [GOAG: Generative and Object-Agnostic Grasp Planner for Dexterous Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19759)
*Julien Merand,Boris Meden,Mathieu Grossard,Liming Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GOAG是一种生成式、对象无关的灵巧手抓取规划器。它基于"夹爪与物体在接触点共享相同表面几何"这一观察，学习特定夹爪接触表面的紧凑潜在分布，仅推理时引入物体特征即可采样有效抓取配置，无需依赖物体特定训练数据。在MultiDex数据集上平均成功率达86.93%，达到甚至匹配针对该数据集专门训练的最先进方法的性能，且生成大量抓取时速度显著更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指抓取是机器人关键技能，但现有基于深度学习的抓取规划器由于在有限的、物体特定数据集上训练，难以泛化到新物体。作者希望突破这一局限，提出一种不依赖物体特定训练数据的对象无关方法。

Method: GOAG基于"夹爪与物体在接触点表面几何一致"的核心观察，构建深度生成模型学习特定夹爪接触表面的紧凑潜在分布（即夹爪可实现的接触区域分布），在无需物体特定训练的前提下高效采样有效抓取配置。物体特征仅在推理阶段引入，用于检索与夹爪能力兼容的有效接触区域。

Result: 在仿真与真实场景上借助多种不同夹爪进行了大量实验验证。在MultiDex数据集上平均成功率86.93%，达到最先进水平；生成大量抓取时处理速度显著更快，同时性能匹配专门在数据集上训练的领先方法。

Conclusion: GOAG展示了对象无关学习的优势，有效解决了传统数据驱动抓取规划器的泛化难题，不依赖物体特定训练数据即可实现与专用方法相当甚至更优的抓取性能，为灵巧手抓取提供了一条全新的泛化路径。

Abstract: Multifingered grasping is a crucial robotic skill, but current deep-learning grasp planners often struggle to generalize to new objects because they are trained on limited, object-specific datasets. We introduce a fundamentally different approach, grounded in the observation that the gripper and the object share identical surface geometry at their mutual contact points. We propose GOAG: Generative and Object-Agnostic Grasp Planner for Dexterous Robotic Manipulation, a novel deep generative model that learns a compact latent representation of a specific gripper's contact surface distribution, enabling the efficient sampling of valid grasp configurations without relying on object-specific training data. We show that by introducing object features only at inference time, our model can effectively retrieve admissible contact areas that are compatible with the gripper's capabilities. We validate our approach through extensive experiments on established grasp protocols in both simulated and real-world scenarios, demonstrating its effectiveness with different grippers from the literature. Our method delivers state-of-the-art results on the objects from the MultiDex dataset, achieving an average success rate of 86.93%. It offers significantly faster processing when generating numerous grasps, while matching the performance of leading approaches specifically trained on this dataset. Unlike these methods, our approach does not rely on object-specific training data, highlighting the advantages of object-agnostic learning. It effectively addresses the generalization challenges faced by traditional data-driven grasp planners. Code and videos are available on our project website https://cea-list.github.io/goagweb/ .

</details>


### [147] [CoToGrasp: Contact-Topology-Conditioned Dexterous Grasp Synthesis via Canonical Workspace Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19776)
*Julien Merand,Boris Meden,Liming Chen,Mathieu Grossard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoToGrasp是一个新颖的生成式框架，能够在对象无关的条件下合成严格基于特定接触拓扑的多样化、稳定抓取。通过引入基于特征的标准工作空间，将局部对象特征投影到统一的多指夹持器中心域，从而将语义功能意图与任意对象几何解耦。该模型在大型DexGraspNet数据集上实现了最先进的性能，并具备对未见过对象的零样本泛化能力，同时在物理机器人平台上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的灵巧抓取规划器主要优化物理稳定性，聚焦于"能否抓住对象"而非"如何抓取以支持下游功能性任务"。然而，基于特定人手抓取分类法来约束抓取合成通常需要昂贵且带对象标注的数据集，这构成了数据瓶颈。

Method: 提出CoToGrasp生成式框架：(1)以完全对象无关的方式训练，绕过数据采集瓶颈；(2)引入特征化规范工作空间，将局部对象特征投影到统一的夹持器中心域，解耦语义功能意图与对象几何；(3)在规范工作空间内学习夹持器的内在接触流形，实现对未见过对象的零样本泛化。

Result: 在大型DexGraspNet数据集上的广泛评估表明，CoToGrasp实现了最先进的性能，优于现有分类法引导的规划器；并在物理机器人平台上验证了合成接触拓扑的物理可行性与运动学可行性。

Conclusion: 通过对象无关的训练策略和特征化规范工作空间的引入，CoToGrasp成功实现了在无昂贵标注数据条件下精确控制抓取接触拓扑的合成，并验证了其在实际机器人系统中的可用性。

Abstract: Current dexterous grasp planners primarily optimize for physical stability, focusing on whether an object can be grasped rather than how it should be grasped to support downstream functional tasks. However, conditioning grasp synthesis on specific human grasp taxonomies typically requires prohibitively expensive, object-annotated datasets. To address these limitations, we propose CoToGrasp, a novel generative framework that synthesizes diverse, stable grasps strictly conditioned on specific contact topologies. To bypass the data collection bottleneck, CoToGrasp is trained entirely in an object-agnostic manner. We introduce a feature-based canonical workspace that projects local object features into a unified gripper-centric domain, effectively decoupling the semantic functional intent from the arbitrary object geometry. By learning the intrinsic contact manifold of the gripper within this workspace, our model achieves zero-shot generalization to unseen objects at inference. Extensive evaluations on the large-scale DexGraspNet dataset demonstrate that CoToGrasp achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming existing taxonomy-guided planners. Finally, we demonstrate the physical viability and kinematic feasibility of our synthesized contact topologies on a physical robot platform. Code is available on our project website https://cea-list.github.io/cotograspweb/ .

</details>


### [148] [MILD: Tractable Terrain Modeling for Learning Improved Bipedal Locomotion on Deformable Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19955)
*Zeren Luo,Jiahui Zhang,Zhe Xu,Wanyue Li,Xinqi Li,Xuechao Chen,Zhangguo Yu,Annan Tang,Peng Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了MILD框架，包含基于物理的离散元接触求解器用于精确模拟可变形地面上的足部-地形交互，并结合深度强化学习训练地形感知的步态控制器，通过硬件实验验证了在线地形识别与自适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双足机器人在可变形（松软）地面上行走至关重要（灾难响应、行星探索等），但现有仿真器无法捕获此类地面在时空上的异质性，限制了双足机器人在可变形表面的运动能力发展。

Method: 1) 提出MILD框架，采用物理基础的离散元接触求解器精确模拟空间变化的足部-地形交互；2) 通过深度强化学习训练地形感知控制器，引入潜变量调制与本体感知估计。

Result: 与最先进方法相比，该方法在训练中生成更多样、更真实的接触场景，所训练控制器在真实可变形表面上展现出自然的适应性；硬件实验验证了系统能够在线识别地形并在广泛的表面刚度范围内自适应。

Conclusion: 物理基础的离散元仿真结合强化学习训练的地形感知控制器，能够帮助双足机器人在可变形地面上实现自然、自适应的行走，具备在线地形识别与适应能力。

Abstract: Enabling robots to walk on yielding terrain is vital for applications ranging from disaster response to planetary exploration. While bipedal robots hold immense potential, their locomotion on deformable surfaces remains limited as current simulators fail to capture the spatiotemporal heterogeneity of such yielding substrates. We present MILD, featuring a physics-grounded discrete-element contact solver that accurately simulates spatially varying foot-terrain interactions. Complementing this model, we train a terrain-aware locomotion controller via deep reinforcement learning with latent modulation and proprioceptive estimation. Quantitative comparisons against state-of-the-art methods show our approach generates more diverse and realistic contact scenarios during training, resulting in controllers that exhibit natural adaptation on real deformable surfaces. Through hardware experiments, we demonstrate the system's capability for online terrain identification and adaptation across a wide range of surface stiffness.

</details>


### [149] [PVRA: A Pointwise Key-point Voting Framework for Robotic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19968)
*Kulunu Samarawickrama,Roel Pieters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对机器人自主渐进式装配操作问题，通过比较分析相关领域研究，提出目标中心感知需向学习装配依赖关系方向演进。作者提出一个基于3D关键点的模块化学习框架，从RGB-D输入中学习装配依赖关系并推断可操作输出，并在装配姿态估计数据集上训练评估，使用增强的渐进式装配指标与目标中心基线进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代计算机视觉虽已实现机器人装配操作的部分自主性，但进行渐进式装配的自主操作需要更具体的技能集合而不仅仅是物体感知。现有目标中心感知方法不足以支撑渐进式装配，需要向学习装配依赖关系以预测有意义的可操作输出方向推进。

Method: 提出一个基于3D关键点的模块化学习框架，从RGB-D输入中学习装配依赖关系并推断可操作输出。该框架通过模块化设计将装配依赖学习与可操作输出预测解耦，输入为装配场景的RGB-D数据。

Result: 在装配姿态估计数据集上训练并评估了该网络，并与目标中心基线方法进行了对比，使用为渐进式装配设计的增强指标集进行评估。

Conclusion: 通过比较分析表明，目标中心感知必须向学习装配依赖方向演进，才能实现有效的自主渐进式装配操作；所提出的基于3D关键点的模块化框架是一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern computer vision has enabled partial autonomy in robotic assembly manipulation. However, performing autonomous manipulation of a progressive assembly demands a more specific set of skills, in addition to perceiving the objects. Through a comparative analysis of research in the associated domains, we deduce that object-centric perception must advance towards learning assembly dependencies to predict meaningful actionable outputs for autonomous assembly manipulation. Subsequently, we present a 3D keypoint-based modular learning framework to learn assembly dependencies to infer actionable outputs given a RGB-D input of an assembly scene. We train and evaluate our trained network on an assembly pose estimation dataset and compare it against object-centric baselines with an augmented set of metrics for progressive assemblies.

</details>


### [150] [Learning Highly Dynamic Skills Transition for Quadruped Jumping Through Constrained Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19977)
*Zeren Luo,Jiahui Zhang,Yimin Han,Ji Ma,Minghao Lu,Ioannis Havoutis,Peng Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层强化学习流程，使四足机器人能够像动物一样通过狭窄的门等受限障碍物，执行爆发性、侵略性的运动。底层策略通过模仿学习训练真实动物行为，形成多种技能集；高层控制器结合视觉检测的门信息与底层技能能力感知，决策出无碰撞的动态穿越轨迹。该框架还可扩展到其他高动态任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿足动物能够在受限空间内执行爆发性运动，但如何在四足机器人上复现这一行为一直是长期挑战，现有方法难以实现通过狭窄障碍物的激进动态穿越。

Method: 采用分层强化学习框架：（1）底层策略通过模仿学习技术，模仿真实动物的运动行为，形成多样化的技能库；（2）高层控制器利用视觉检测获取门的位置信息，同时感知底层技能的能力边界，选择合适动作并规划无碰撞的动态穿越轨迹。

Result: 该框架使四足机器人能够自主、敏捷地完成空中跨门穿越任务，并验证了该方法可扩展到其他高动态任务中。这是首个在陆地行走机器人上实现自主空中门穿越的研究工作。

Conclusion: 该分层强化学习框架成功使四足机器人的敏捷性接近生物对应物，拓展了腿足机器人逼真的运动能力，证明模仿学习与高层决策相结合是解决激进动态运动控制的有效途径。

Abstract: Although legged animals are capable of performing explosive motions while traversing confined spaces, replicating this behavior in quadrupedal robots has been a longstanding challenge. Here, we propose a hierarchical reinforcement learning pipeline that empowers the robots to perform aggressive locomotion through constrained obstacles--a narrow gate. The imitation learning technique is used to train the low-level policy, which mimics the behaviors of real animals and forms a set of diverse skills. The high-level controller, having an awareness of the capability of low-level skills and acquiring the gate information via vision-based detection, determines the suitable maneuvers with collision-free trajectories to traverse it dynamically. Notably, we also verify that this framework can be extended to other highly dynamic tasks. This is one of the first works that perform autonomous and agile aerial gate traversal tasks on ground-walking robots, extending the lifelike agility of legged robots to match that of their biological counterparts.

</details>


### [151] [Evidence-Gated Task and Motion Planning with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20084)
*Tsunehiko Tanaka,Matthew Stephenson,Alistair Macvicar,Edgar Simo-Serra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EAFG框架，将VLM与TAMP结合，通过VLM生成探索性子目标获取视觉证据，并用可行性门控决定继续规划、获取更多证据或停止，解决部分可观测环境下长期操控任务中目标物体不确定性导致的执行失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测条件下，机器人执行自然语言指令的长程操控任务时，目标物体的可用性存在不确定性。现有VLM+TAMP方法可能依赖VLM先验知识生成缺乏观测支持的子目标，导致执行失败或产生非预期结果。

Method: 提出EAFG框架：首先通过VLM生成探索性子目标，结合TAMP执行来获取视觉证据；然后应用可行性门控（feasibility gate）决策下一步是继续任务规划、获取更多证据还是停止执行。

Result: 在物体用途模糊的烹饪任务中，EAFG通过规划前发现任务相关物体提升了配方完成率；对于需要缺失物体的指令，EAFG促进合理的停止决策并减少对该物体的重复操作尝试。

Conclusion: EAFG通过在VLM与TAMP之间引入证据获取与可行性门控机制，有效缓解了部分可观测环境中VLM先验知识与实际观测不一致导致的任务失败，同时提升了任务完成率和指令安全性。

Abstract: Robots executing long-horizon manipulation tasks from natural-language instructions must reason about both semantic task structure and geometric feasibility. However, under partial observability, the availability of goal-relevant objects may be uncertain. In such cases, approaches that combine Vision-Language Models (VLMs) with Task and Motion Planning (TAMP) may generate subgoals that rely on the VLM's prior knowledge without observational support, leading to execution failures or unintended outcomes. We propose Evidence Acquisition and Feasibility Gating (EAFG), a framework that acquires visual evidence through VLM-generated exploratory subgoals and TAMP-based execution. EAFG then applies a feasibility gate to decide whether to proceed with task planning, acquire further evidence, or halt. Our experiments show that, in cooking tasks with ambiguous object use, EAFG improves recipe completion by discovering task-relevant objects before planning. For instructions requiring an absent object, EAFG promotes appropriate halt decisions and reduces repeated attempts to manipulate that object.

</details>


### [152] [Towards Professional Tennis Styles for Humanoid Robots with Adaptive Motion Planning and Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20087)
*Tao Huang,Ruofei Liu,Xuchen Tang,Xinyin Zhang,Junli Ren,Huayi Wang,Feiyu Jia,Yukai Qi,Kangning Yin,Weishuai Zeng,Lipeng Chen,Xi Li,Ting Wu,Kailin Li,Ruoli Dai,Jingbo Wang,Lei Han,Jiangmiao Pang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AdaPT是一个自适应运动规划与跟踪框架，直接从播放大赛中学习专业网球发球和回合动作，通过层级设计（规划器生成风格化运动，跟踪器最小干扰执行）以及针对sim-to-real差距的适应机制，在Unitree G1和全尺寸Dobit Atom人形机器人上实现了野外发球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation:  人形机器人在真实世界球类运动中已有初步能力，但如何在保持强大任务性能的同时实现专业运动风格仍然具有挑战性。此外，仿真中规划的优雅动作在真实机器人上执行时面临sim-to-real差距：跟踪性能必然退化，自回归规划部分忽略这种退化，且被噪声感知进一步加剧。

Method: AdaPT采用层级架构：规划器从广播视频中学习专业网球风格的运动学动作，跟踪器则最小化对规划的干扰。为了解决sim-to-real差距，适应机制通过学习跟踪随机化的执行速度来提升跟踪鲁棒性，同时将规划器条件化在学习的运动速度适配器上以缓解规划中的累积误差。

Result: 在Unitree G1真实机器人上验证了适应机制能有效弥合sim-to-real差距；并在全尺寸Dobit Atom人形机器人（1.7m）上部署AdaPT策略，展示了无需动作捕捉的野外发球能力。

Conclusion: 通过真实世界的实验，AdaPT不仅验证了层级规划-跟踪框架和适应机制的有效性，还揭示了未来人形球类运动系统的算法与工程见解。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have recently demonstrated promising capabilities in real-world ball sports. However, achieving professional motion styles while maintaining strong task performance remains challenging. In this work, we propose AdaPT, an Adaptive Motion Planning and Tracking framework that learns professional tennis serving and rally styles directly from broadcast videos. This hierarchical design is motivated by the key insight that the planner generates stylistic kinematic motions, while the tracker executes them with minimal interference with planning. Despite its effectiveness in simulation, a substantial sim-to-real gap emerges: tracking performance inevitably degrades on real robots, and this degradation is partially overlooked by autoregressive planning and further compounded by noisy perception. To address these issues, our adaptation mechanism improves tracking robustness by learning to track randomized execution speeds, while conditioning the planner on a learned motion-speed adapter to mitigate compounding errors. Real-world experiments on the Unitree G1 demonstrate the effectiveness of our adaptation mechanism in bridging the sim-to-real gap. We further deploy AdaPT policies on the full-size Dobot Atom humanoid robot (1.7m) and demonstrate in-the-wild serving without motion capture. Beyond these results, our real-world experiments reveal both algorithmic and engineering insights for future humanoid ball-sports systems. Videos and code are available on our \href{https://humanoidtennis.github.io/AdaPT/}{project website}.

</details>


### [153] [Planning-Oriented End-to-End Autonomous Driving: Architectures, Evaluation, and Emerging Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20111)
*Yanchen Guan,Xingcheng Liu,Bin Rao,Chengyue Wang,Guofa Li,Yunjian Li,Lishengsa Yue,Zhiyong Cui,Chengzhong Xu,Zhenning Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了端到端自动驾驶从相机到控制回归向规划导向系统演进的历程，提出现代端到端驾驶的关键区别不在于是否使用中间表示，而在于这些表示是否被学习、监督和评估以支持安全、可行和符合路线的规划。论文沿输入表示、规划输出、监督信号和评估协议四个轴组织文献，并分析了从开环轨迹匹配到闭环仿真等基准测试的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶领域快速发展，产生了从行为克隆到视觉-语言-动作系统等多种范式，但缺乏系统的综述来厘清这些方法之间的本质区别，特别是难以解读架构进展在不同评估协议下的真实表现。

Method: 作者对端到端自动驾驶文献进行了系统性综述，沿四个维度组织：输入表示（如BEV、向量化表示）、规划输出（轨迹级）、监督信号（行为克隆、条件模仿学习、特权蒸馏）、评估协议（开环与闭环）。同时分析了基准从开环轨迹匹配向闭环仿真、非反应式真实日志评估、长尾测试和人类偏好感知指标的转移。

Result: 分析表明，缺乏基准一致性的评估使得架构进展难以解读；仅依赖基于位移的开环指标（如L2误差）无法充分证明驾驶的安全性和与人类行为的对齐性。综述揭示了从纯传感器-控制回归向结构化表示、轨迹输出和更真实评估协议的转变趋势。

Conclusion: 作者总结了端到端自动驾驶面临的关键开放挑战，包括：不确定性感知规划、学习器-专家不匹配问题、运行时安全保障、语言-动作接地、世界模型验证以及可复现的基准评估。这些挑战指向未来研究需要在评估协议标准化和规划安全保证方面取得突破。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving has evolved from camera-to-control regression toward planning-oriented systems that use structured representations, trajectory-level outputs, and increasingly realistic evaluation protocols. This survey reviews this transition across behavior cloning, conditional imitation learning, privileged distillation, BEV and vectorized planning, unified perception-prediction-planning architectures, world-model-based planners, and vision-language-action systems. We argue that the key distinction in modern end-to-end driving is not whether intermediate representations are used, but whether they are learned, supervised, and evaluated to support safe, feasible, and route-compliant planning. To organize the literature, we synthesize existing methods along four axes: input representation, planning output, supervision signal, and evaluation protocol. We further examine the benchmark shift from open-loop trajectory matching to closed-loop simulation, non-reactive real-log evaluation, long-tail testing, and human-preference-aware metrics. Our analysis highlights that architectural progress is difficult to interpret without benchmark-consistent evaluation, and that displacement-based open-loop metrics alone provide limited evidence for safe and human-aligned driving. We conclude with open challenges in uncertainty-aware planning, learner-expert mismatch, runtime safety assurance, language-action grounding, world-model validation, and reproducible benchmarking.

</details>


### [154] [Video2DoorTraversal: Push Door Traversal via Simulated Door Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20251)
*Xincheng Tang,Yiji Chen,Youhan Xie,Wanyu Li,Zhengjie Shu,Lai Jiang,Wenkang Hu,Yitong Li,Jinchuang Zhang,Xibin Song,Ruigang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Video2DoorTraversal是一个面向轮腿式移动机械臂的单视频"真实-仿真-真实"框架，可从一段真实门的RGB视频中重建仿真门孪生体，基于仿真训练物理可执行的开门与穿越演示，并训练ArticuACT策略实现机载自主的门开关与穿越任务，在五扇真实门上达到96.57%平均成功率，在结构相似未见过门上实现80.95%零样本成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开门与穿越是典型的长时程移动-操作(loco-manipulation)任务，涉及精确的把手交互与底盘-手臂协同控制，对现实世界中的技能学习与策略部署提出高难度挑战。

Method: 提出真实到仿真到真实(real-to-sim-to-real)的单视频框架：1) DoorTwin从单RGB视频重建实例对齐、带铰链关节且仿真就绪的门孪生体，具有真实几何与外观；2) 仿真在环(simulation-in-the-loop)智能体将关节信息转化为参数化技能程序，并通过迭代优化失败回放生成物理可执行演示；3) ArticuACT是一种双深度策略，基于机器人中心相机条件化与交互感知监督，联合预测底盘、手臂和夹爪指令。

Result: 所有感知与策略推理均机载运行，在五扇真实门上平均成功率达96.57%，在结构相似的未见门上零样本成功率达80.95%，完整接近-开门-穿越流程平均约13秒完成。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了仅凭单段视频即可完成真机门开关与穿越技能的高效迁移，验证了仿真驱动的长时程移动操作策略在零样本泛化上的可行性。

Abstract: Door opening and traversal is a long-horizon loco-manipulation task that requires precise handle interaction and coordinated base-arm control. We present Video2DoorTraversal, a single-video real-to-sim-to-real framework for wheel-legged mobile manipulators. Given one RGB video of a real door, DoorTwin reconstructs an instance-aligned, articulated, and simulation-ready door twin with realistic geometry and appearance. A simulation-in-the-loop agent converts the recovered articulation into a parameterized skill program and iteratively refines failed rollouts to generate physically executable demonstrations. These demonstrations are used to train ArticuACT, a dual-depth policy that predicts coordinated base, arm, and gripper commands using robot-centric camera conditioning and interaction-aware supervision. With all perception and policy inference running onboard, the system achieves a 96.57% average success rate across five real doors and an 80.95% zero-shot success rate on structurally similar unseen doors, while completing the full approach, opening, and traversal sequence in approximately 13s on average. Project Page: https://video2doortraversal.github.io/.

</details>


### [155] [DART-S: Reachability-Audited Active-Suspension Preconditioning for Off-Road Vehicle Jumps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20275)
*Yu Hu,Fangzhou Zhao,Liang Chen,Chen Min,Wei Li,Mingyuan Sang,Jiajia Ma,Shican Chen,Di Pang,Baolei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DART-S通过对斜坡面悬挂预调节来改变起飞前的俯仰角、俯仰率和车轮自旋，从而将空中扭矩反应无法纠正的起飞误差转化为可管理的状态预算。通过局部校准映射和支撑感知选择器，在600次新运行中实现所有正负和边界查询的精确分支行为，显著优于基线DART方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中扭矩反作用力无法在车轮角动量预算内纠正起飞误差，这是一种根本性的物理限制。现有方法（如DART）在超出预算的情况下无法实现期望的起飞后姿态，因此需要一种能在起飞前就改变系统状态和剩余权限预算的方法。

Method: DART-S采用斜坡面悬挂预调节方法，在起飞前改变俯仰角、俯仰率和车轮自旋。具体包括：1) 局部校准映射用于预测每次悬挂动作如何重塑状态-预算对；2) 支撑感知选择器结合预测偏移、局部结果证据和区间可达性筛选；3) 精确配对审计报告剩余权限。

Result: 在72个独立BeamNG会话中的600次新运行中，所有正负和边界查询均遵循预定分支。在40°/13 m/s边界处实现24/24姿态标准成功（DART为0/24，会话层面Holm校正p=0.0234）；在11.5 m/s下0.35秒定时动作实现23/24成功率（静态预设为0/24，p=0.0156）；200 rad/s命令防护在所有600次运行中保持传动系统硬限零越界。

Conclusion: DART-S通过起飞前悬挂预调节显著扩展了起飞误差的可纠正范围，在所有查询类型和边界条件下均展现了优于基线方法的统计显著性能，同时保证了系统的安全性。

Abstract: Airborne torque reaction cannot recover takeoff errors beyond the wheel angular-momentum budget. DART-S applies ramp-face suspension preconditioning to change pitch, pitch rate, and wheel spin before liftoff, thereby shifting the queried state and altering the remaining authority budget. To predict how each suspension action reshapes this state-budget pair, DART-S employs a local calibration map. A support-aware selector combines the predicted shift with local outcome evidence and an interval-reachability screen; an exact-pair audit reports residual authority. Across 600 new runs in 72 independent BeamNG sessions, every positive, negative, and boundary query follows its prespecified branch. At the confirmed 40°/13 m/s boundary, DART-S attains 24/24 post-touchdown attitude-criterion successes versus 0/24 for DART (session-level Holm-adjusted p=0.0234). At 11.5 m/s, a 0.35 s timing action attains 23/24 versus 0/24 for the static preset (p=0.0156). The 200 rad/s command guard keeps drivetrain hard-limit exceedance at zero across all 600 runs. The source code will be available at https://github.com/MeridianCAS/DART-S

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Deep Learning for Automated Quantification of Tumor-Associated Macrophages from H&E-Stained Slides in Diffuse Large B-Cell Lymphoma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19283)
*Anastasiia Studenikina,Svetlana Illarionova,Joaquim Carreras,Daniil Sulimov,Olga Filimonova,Dmitry Zvezdin,Arsenii Galimov,Ivan Tyukin,Rifat Hamoudi,Maxim Sharaev*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了利用深度学习直接从常规HE染色组织切片中量化M2型肿瘤相关巨噬细胞（TAMs）的可行性，以替代资源密集型的CD163免疫组化（IHC）检测。在52例弥漫性大B细胞淋巴瘤（DLBCL）患者中，评估了五种深度学习架构，其中基于Transformer的Swin-U-Net在分割保真度上表现最优（F1=0.633），其预测的CD163水平与IHC高密度阈值（>20%）相近的20.8%截断点，且高CD163密度与显著降低的总生存期（HR 2.73）和无进展生存期（HR 2.88）相关，表明深度学习方法有望成为可扩展且经济高效的IHC替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: M2型肿瘤相关巨噬细胞是DLBCL中疾病侵袭性的重要指标，但传统CD163免疫组化检测资源密集、成本高昂。研究旨在探索利用深度学习直接从常规HE染色切片中量化TAMs的可行性，以实现低成本、可扩展的预后评估。

Method: 使用52例DLBCL患者的高分辨率HE图像及IHC验证的注释数据集（1,713个TAM实例），评估五种深度学习架构：U-Net、Swin-U-Net、Cerberus-U-Net3+、YOLOv11和HoVer-Net。通过生存分析（Kaplan-Meier和Cox比例风险模型）验证CD163 TAM密度的预后价值，并与模型预测结果进行对比。

Result: 高CD163 TAM密度（>20.04%）与显著降低的总生存期（HR 2.73; p=0.012）和无进展生存期（HR 2.88; p=0.011）相关。多变量分析中显示预后趋势（OS HR 3.24, p=0.062; PFS HR 2.41, p=0.120）。Cerberus-U-Net3+获得最高召回率（0.656），Swin-U-Net获得最优精确度（0.694）和F1分数（0.633）。基于Swin-U-Net预测的20.8%截断点与IHC的20%阈值接近，高预测值患者OS呈下降趋势（HR 2.63; p=0.083）。

Conclusion: 采用移位窗口自注意力机制的深度学习架构（特别是Swin-U-Net）展现出作为IHC替代方案的潜力，其可扩展性和成本效益使其有望成为DLBCL中TAMs预后评估的候选工具，但需进一步验证。

Abstract: While M2-polarized tumor-associated macrophages (TAMs) have been established as indicators of disease aggressiveness in diffuse large B-cell lymphoma (DLBCL), traditional CD163 immunohistochemistry (IHC) remains resource-intensive. This study aims to investigate the feasibility of using deep learning to quantify TAMs directly from standard hematoxylin and eosin-stained (HE) tissue sections. Using a curated dataset of 52 patients with DLBCL, with high-resolution HE images and IHC-validated annotations (1,713 TAM instances), five architectures were evaluated: U-Net, Swin-U-Net, Cerberus-U-Net3+, YOLOv11, and HoVer-Net. High CD163 TAM density (>20.04%) was associated with significantly reduced overall survival (HR 2.73; 95% CI:1.21-6.16; p=0.012) and progression-free survival (HR 2.88; 95% CI: 1.23-6.76; p=0.011). In multivariate Cox proportional hazards analysis adjusting for IPI, molecular subtype (GCB/non-GCB per Hans algorithm), EBV status, and age, CD163 TAM density showed a prognostic trend for overall survival (HR 3.24; 95% CI: 0.94-11.15; p=0.062) and progression-free survival (HR 2.41; 95% CI: 0.80-7.32; p=0.120). Among the evaluated models, the domain-specific Cerberus-U-Net3+ achieved the highest sensitivity (Recall 0.656), while the Transformer-based Swin-U-Net demonstrated superior segmentation fidelity (Precision 0.694, F1-score 0.633). Additionally, the survival analysis based on the predicted Swin-U-Net CD163 level revealed a 20.8% cutoff point for patients with high and low CD163 levels near IHC, as well as a downward trend in overall survival among patients with higher predicted CD163 values of 2.63 (95% CI: 0.85-8.33; p=0.083). These findings suggest that deep learning architectures using shifted-window self-attention show potential as a candidate surrogate for IHC that is scalable and cost-effective for prognostic assessment of TAMs in DLBCL.

</details>


### [157] [MOSAIC: A Self-supervised Dynamic Multi-encoding Reconstruction Framework for 3D Late Gadolinium Enhancement MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19506)
*Muhammad A. Sultan,Yingmin Liu,Katherine Binzel,Katarzyna E. Gil,Karolina M. Zareba,Rizwan Ahmad*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种名为MOSAIC的自监督动态重建框架，用于高度欠采样的双回波三维晚期钆增强心脏MRI，在加速因子超过1000的情况下实现运动分辨的自由呼吸成像，并优于现有先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3D LGE MRI重建方法依赖全采样训练数据集或预计算的灵敏度图，且现有方法将采集数据分箱到不同运动状态进行处理，难以在高度欠采样条件下同时建模多回波图像内容、线圈灵敏度图和逐心跳非刚性运动。需要一种无需全采样数据即可实现高加速因子下运动分辨重建的方法。

Method: MOSAIC框架联合建模多回波图像内容、线圈灵敏度图及逐心跳非刚性运动，直接从欠采样数据中进行自监督重建。与现有分箱方法不同，MOSAIC从每个心跳重建运动分辨的3D LGE图像。评估采用数字体模（含模拟心肌疤痕）及活体动物和人体研究，对比低秩深度图像先验重建及消融变体。

Result: 体模实验中，MOSAIC的PSNR和SSIM均高于低秩深度图像先验重建及其消融变体。动物和人类研究中，MOSAIC获得更高的盲法专家图像质量评分，优于图像导航压缩感知和低秩深度图像先验重建。整体实现了超过1000倍的加速因子，同时改善了细节保留和伪影抑制。

Conclusion: MOSAIC证明了在加速因子超过1000的条件下实现运动分辨自由呼吸双回波3D LGE MRI的可行性，相对于最先进对比方法在细节保留和伪影抑制方面均有显著提升。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop and evaluate a self-supervised dynamic reconstruction framework for highly undersampled dual-echo three-dimensional late gadolinium enhancement (3D LGE) MRI.
  Methods: MOSAIC jointly models multi-echo image content, coil sensitivity maps, and beat-specific nonrigid motion directly from acquired undersampled data, without requiring fully sampled training datasets or accurate precomputed sensitivity maps. Unlike existing methods that bin the acquired data into different motion states, with or without motion compensation, MOSAIC reconstructs a motion-resolved 3D LGE image from each heartbeat. The method was evaluated using digital phantoms with simulated myocardial scars and in vivo animal and human studies.
  Results: In phantom experiments, MOSAIC achieved higher peak signal-to-noise ratio and structural similarity index measure than low-rank deep image prior reconstruction and ablation variants of MOSAIC. In animal and human studies, MOSAIC achieved higher blinded expert image-quality scores than inline image-navigated compressed-sensing and low-rank deep image prior reconstructions.
  Conclusion: MOSAIC demonstrated the feasibility of motion-resolved free-breathing dual-echo 3D LGE MRI at acceleration factors exceeding 1,000, with improved detail preservation and artifact suppression relative to the state-of-the-art comparison methods.

</details>


### [158] [Loss-Resilient Semantic Communication over Packet-Loss Networks at Extreme-Low Bandwidth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19590)
*Shengshi Yao,Jincheng Dai,Sixian Wang,Guo Lu,Kai Niu,Wenjun Xu,Wenjun Zhang,Ping Zhang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出ResiGLC，一个针对极低带宽丢包网络下的鲁棒生成式潜在编码框架。利用语言模型的内在上下文预测能力并结合掩码学习策略，支持潜在码的任意上下文建模以缓解错误传播；在接收端设计渐进式弹性解码管线，同时利用潜在码的上下文关系和生成潜在空间中的多模态语义先验，实现压缩效率与丢包鲁棒性的联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式语义编解码器在极低带宽下虽能有效降低视觉通信的带宽成本，但仅针对压缩效率优化，缺乏对传输错误的鲁棒性。信道丢包导致的压缩潜在表征损坏会引发严重保真度和真实感退化，且错误会在潜在上下文和多步解码过程中严重传播。因此亟需设计具有丢包鲁棒性的生成式潜在编码框架。

Method: 1) 利用生成与压缩目标一致性，充分发挥语言模型的上下文预测能力；2) 集成掩码学习策略，支持潜在码的任意上下文建模，缓解错误传播并处理不可预测的丢包模式；3) 接收端设计渐进式弹性解码管线，分别利用潜在码的上下文关系与生成潜在空间中的多模态语义先验；4) 联合优化压缩效率与丢包弹性。

Result: 大量实验证明，在丢包网络条件下，ResiGLC在感知保真度和真实感质量方面显著提升了丢包鲁棒性，同时保持极低带宽成本。

Conclusion: ResiGLC通过融合语言模型上下文建模和掩码学习，结合渐进式解码机制，实现了压缩效率与丢包鲁棒性的良好权衡，为极低带宽丢包网络中的鲁棒语义通信提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: In extreme-low bandwidth network scenarios, generative semantic codecs have emerged as promising solutions to reduce bandwidth cost for visual communications. However, these learned codecs are usually optimized solely for compression efficiency and thus not robust against transmission errors. Corruptions due to packet-loss among these highly compact generative latent representations often cause more critical degradation in fidelity and realism, intensified by the severe error propagation across the latent contexts and multi-step decoding process. In this paper, we propose ResiGLC, a novel loss-resilient generative latent coding framework designed for robust semantic communication over extreme-low bandwidth packet-loss networks. Motivated by the inherent goal-consistency between generation and compression, we sufficiently exploit the impressive in-context predictive capabilities of language models. Integrated with the masked learning strategy, our model supports arbitrary context modeling of latent codes, which could mitigate the error propagation and handle unpredictable packet loss patterns. At the receiver, a progressive resilient decoding pipeline is presented, which leverages both the contextual relationship of the latent codes and the multi-modal semantic prior in the generative latent space, separately. By jointly optimizing toward both compression efficiency and packet-loss resilience, our proposed progressive decoding mechanism offers graceful performance when dealing with dynamic packet losses. Through extensive experimental evaluations, we establish that under packet-loss network conditions, ResiGLC can effectively improve the loss-resilience in terms of perceptual fidelity and realism qualities with extreme-low bandwidth cost.

</details>


### [159] [AsymFeX: A Symmetry-Driven Framework for Ischemic Stroke Segmentation Across Imaging Modalities and Stroke Stages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19769)
*Maunil Shah,Vaanathi Sundaresan*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于对称性的两阶段三维分割方法用于急性缺血性卒中（AIS）病灶分割，第一阶段校正头部倾斜使其对齐真实解剖正中矢状面，第二阶段引入新型非对称特征提取（AsymFeX）模块，通过跨半球注意力、特征差异估计和双尺度门控在局部3x3x3邻域内比较每个体素与其对侧对应体素。在AISD数据集上达到0.6796 Dice、23.53 mm HD95和7.69 mL AVD，显著优于现有方法，并在ATLAS v2.1和ISLES'24上验证了跨模态、跨时间点的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非增强CT（NCCT）是诊断缺血性梗死的首选影像学方式，但其梗死对比度低，导致手动勾画耗时费力且主观性强。受临床实践中通过比较两侧大脑半球来定位梗死灶的启发，作者提出利用脑部对称性进行自动分割，以提高AIS病灶分割的准确性和效率，为卒中预后和治疗决策（如70 mL溶栓阈值评估）提供可靠支持。

Method: 方法包含两个阶段：（1）第一阶段通过头部倾斜校正将扫描对齐到真实解剖正中矢状面；（2）第二阶段使用与nnU-Net兼容的3D分割框架，核心是AsymFeX模块，该模块在局部3x3x3邻域内将每个体素与其真实对侧对应体素进行比较，通过跨半球注意力、特征差异估计和双尺度门控机制同时捕获大小不同的梗死灶。

Result: 在AISD数据集上，该方法达到0.6796 Dice、23.53 mm HD95和7.69 mL AVD，显著超越现有最先进方法；在70 mL溶栓适宜性阈值上进行了临床相关的体积分析。在ATLAS v2.1和ISLES'24上进行的验证性实验表明，该对称驱动设计无需架构改动即可跨成像模态和卒中时间点泛化，不确定性分析进一步评估了临床部署下的可靠性。

Conclusion: 基于大脑对称性的自动分割策略能有效提升NCCT上AIS病灶分割的性能，且具有良好的跨数据集泛化能力，为临床卒中评估和溶栓决策提供了有潜力的自动化工具。代码已公开可复现。

Abstract: Fast and accurate segmentation of Acute Ischemic Stroke (AIS) lesions is essential for stroke prognosis and treatment planning. Non-contrast CT (NCCT), the first-line imaging modality for diagnosing ischemic infarcts, exhibits subtle infarct contrast, making manual delineation slow and labor-intensive. Motivated by this, and by the clinical practice of comparing brain hemispheres to localize infarcts, we propose a two-stage, nnU-Net-compatible 3D segmentation method. The first stage corrects head tilt to align each scan to its true anatomical mid-sagittal plane; the second applies a novel Asymmetric Feature Extraction (AsymFeX) module, comparing each voxel to its true contralateral counterpart within a local 3 x 3 x 3 neighborhood via cross-hemispheric attention, feature disparity estimation, and dual-scale gating to capture both large and small infarcts. On AISD, our method achieves 0.6796 Dice, 23.53 mm HD95, and 7.69 mL AVD, significantly outperforming existing state-of-the-art methods, with clinically relevant volumetric analysis at the 70 mL thrombolysis-eligibility threshold. Proof-of-concept evaluation on ATLAS v2.1 and ISLES'24 demonstrates that the same symmetry-driven design generalizes across imaging modalities and stroke time points without architectural changes, further supported by an uncertainty analysis assessing reliability under clinical deployment. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/biomedia-lab/AIS-detection.

</details>


### [160] [MOSAIC: Modality-agnostic Spectral Alignment for Federated Image-level Weakly Supervised Tumor Segmentation under Client-specific Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19788)
*Tarun Kumar Garg,Vaanathi Sundaresan*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: MOSAIC是首个面向客户端特定缺失模态的模态无关联邦弱监督二值肿瘤分割框架。它通过客户端特定模态对齐模块将可用通道融合到共享潜空间，利用谱原型对齐损失协调跨客户端分布偏移，并使用联邦精炼网络去噪CAM伪标签。在三个多机构脑肿瘤基准（FeTS2022、BraTS-MEN、BraTS-SSA）上超越所有图像/框/点监督基线，仅用图像级标签即接近全监督精度（FeTS2022上Dice达0.84），并支持动态新客户端加入（无需重训，Dice损失仅0.01-0.04）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在临床场景中，可信的多模态融合需处理跨机构间不完整且异质的模态子集，同时隐私约束禁止集中式数据共享。联邦学习虽缓解了数据共享限制，但面临客户端特定缺失模态问题——各机构仅拥有不完整的模态子集，导致融合质量和分割性能下降。此前联邦学习与弱监督各自被单独研究，但它们在图像级标签和异质缺失模态下的联合使用仍是空白。

Method: 提出MOSAIC框架，包含三大核心组件：(1)客户端特定模态对齐模块，在未知模态身份先验的情况下将可用通道融合到共享潜空间；(2)谱原型对齐损失，利用紧凑的不可逆频域统计量协调跨客户端的分布偏移；(3)联邦精炼网络，去噪CAM伪标签以生成精确分割掩码，突破弱监督精度上限。

Result: 在三个多机构脑肿瘤基准（FeTS2022、BraTS-MEN、BraTS-SSA）上，MOSAIC显著优于所有图像、框和点监督基线，仅使用图像级标签即可逼近全监督精度，在FeTS2022上达到0.84 Dice。动态新客户端无需重训即可加入已训练联邦，Dice仅下降0.01-0.04。

Conclusion: MOSAIC首次将联邦学习与弱监督结合到客户端特定缺失模态的场景中，通过模态对齐、谱原型对齐和联邦精炼三个模块协同工作，实现了仅用图像级标签即可接近全监督性能的跨机构肿瘤分割，并具备良好的客户端可扩展性。

Abstract: Trustworthy multimodal fusion in clinical settings requires handling incomplete and heterogeneous modality subsets across institutions, where privacy constraints prohibit centralized data sharing. Federated learning (FL) mitigates data-sharing constraints but suffers from client-specific missing modalities, where institutions possess incomplete multimodal subsets, degrading fusion quality and segmentation performance. While FL and weak supervision have been studied separately, their joint use with image-level labels under heterogeneous missing modalities remains unaddressed. We propose \textbf{MOSAIC}, the first modality-agnostic federated framework for weakly supervised binary tumor segmentation under client-specific missing modalities. We introduce a client-specific modality-alignment module that fuses available channels into a shared latent space without prior knowledge of modality identity, a spectral prototype alignment loss that reconciles cross-client distribution shift using compact non-invertible frequency-domain statistics, and a dedicated federated refinement network that denoises the resulting CAM pseudo-labels into accurate masks, breaking the accuracy ceiling of weak supervision. Experiments on three multi-institutional brain tumor benchmarks (FeTS2022, BraTS-MEN, and BraTS-SSA) demonstrate significant improvements over all image, box, and point-supervised baselines, approaching fully supervised accuracy using only image-level labels and reaching 0.84 Dice on FeTS2022. Dynamic new client addition enables previously unseen institutions to join an already-trained federation within 0.01-0.04 Dice without retraining. Code is available at https://github.com/Tarun2201/MOSAIC.

</details>


### [161] [Energy-Mamba: A Physics-Constrained State-Space Model for Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19813)
*Mohamed Mabrok,Yalda Zafari,Essam A. Rashed*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: Energy-Mamba提出了一种将状态空间模型（SSM/Mamba）与物理启发的能量约束相结合的架构，通过可学习的势能函数和基于梯度的强迫项来限制动态隐藏状态演化，防止其在2D医学图像上产生表征漂移，从而在保持线性复杂度的同时提升局部视觉保真度和分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mamba等状态空间模型虽然以线性时间复杂度处理长程依赖，在医学图像（注释数据有限）中很有吸引力，但当将其顺序模型直接适配到2D图像时，无约束的状态演化会导致表征漂移，即动态隐藏状态逐渐丧失对局部图像特征的忠实度。这种漂移在医学成像中尤为致命，因为细粒度的局部细节直接决定诊断准确性。

Method: 引入Energy-Mamba架构，将SSM动力学与物理约束结合。核心创新是Energy-Mamba Block，包含一个可学习的势能函数，用于量化演化状态与静态局部特征之间的兼容性。通过自动微分计算基于梯度的强迫项，将状态推向低能量构型以保持局部视觉保真。该公式类比于哈密顿动力学：SSM扫描提供动能，约束函数提供势能，共同决定状态轨迹。这种架构先验实现学习隐式约束以获得鲁棒、忠实的表征。

Result: 在四个数据集（视网膜OCT、胸部X射线、显微图像、腹部CT）上评估，Energy-Mamba以显著更少的参数量实现了最先进的分类性能，证明物理启发的接地可以同时提升医学视觉任务的效率和表征质量。

Conclusion: 物理信息约束可以有效缓解序列模型在2D医学图像上的表征漂移问题。Energy-Mamba证明了将哈密顿动力学的能量视角引入SSM，不仅增强了模型的表征保真度，还在参数效率上优于现有方法，为医学影像分析中高效、精准的视觉建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: State-Space Models (SSMs), particularly Mamba, offer linear-time complexity for long-range dependencies, making them attractive for medical imaging with limited annotated data. However, adapting these sequential models to 2D images through unconstrained state evolution causes representational drift, the dynamic hidden state progressively loses fidelity to local image features. We introduce Energy-Mamba, integrating SSM dynamics with physics-informed constraints via a learnable potential energy function that quantifies compatibility between evolving states and static local features. Our Energy-Mamba Block introduces a gradient-based forcing term, computed dynamically via automatic differentiation, that pulls states toward low-energy configurations maintaining local visual fidelity. This formulation mirrors Hamiltonian dynamics: kinetic energy (SSM scan) plus potential energy (our constraint function) govern state trajectories. This architectural prior enables learning implicit constraints for robust, faithful representations, crucial in medical imaging where fine-grained local detail drives accurate diagnosis. Evaluated on four datasets (retinal OCT, chest X-ray, microscopy, abdominal CT), Energy-Mamba achieves state-of-the-art classification performance with significantly fewer parameters, demonstrating that physics-informed grounding can enhance both efficiency and representational quality in medical vision tasks.

</details>


### [162] [Simulation-to-Real First-Break Segmentation for Efficient Inversion in Musculoskeletal Ultrasound Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19828)
*Yifei Sun,Yubing Li,Yannick Benezeth,Stéphanie Bricq,Yunrong Zhang,Lekang Jiang,Chang Su,Ligang Cui,Weijun Lin*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种学习辅助的超声CT重建流程，结合基于分割的初至拾取与混合全波形反演（HFWI），通过轻量级2D U-Net利用初至轨迹的空间连续性替代逐道拾取，有效应对骨骼相关散射、衰减和信号退化问题，在体外、离体和在体数据集上均验证了方法的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全波形反演（FWI）用于定量肌骨超声CT重建时，骨骼引起的散射、衰减和信号退化使其对初始声学属性分布高度敏感且易发生循环跳跃。传统逐道初至拾取在弱信号、空间异质或系统噪声干扰下不可靠，难以提供高质量的初始模型。

Method: 提出学习辅助重建流程：（1）使用轻量级2D U-Net将跨接收通道的初至轨迹作为分割目标，利用空间连续性进行初至拾取；（2）结合Rytov近似走时信息与波形拟合的混合全波形反演（HFWI）；（3）采用任务特定仿真预处理（加入真实系统噪声）加渐进退化分阶段训练，以及仅用少量弱标注实验数据对解码器进行微调，以应对仿真到真实的域差距。

Result: 在体外体模、离体牛肢和在体人腿数据集上，相比传统STA/LTA拾取，所提网络获得更空间连续的初至轨迹、更低的平均提取误差，且可在秒级处理全矩阵采集数据。集成到HFWI后，改善初始模型构建并稳定后续FWI重建，即使在局部初至信噪比低于3 dB的挑战性场景下依然稳健。

Conclusion: 基于分割的初至拾取与混合FWI的结合有效解决了超声CT中骨骼引起的反演不稳定性问题，显著提升了弱信号条件下的初始模型质量和重建稳定性，为定量肌骨超声断层成像提供了可行的全流程解决方案。

Abstract: Full-waveform inversion (FWI) is a promising strategy for quantitative musculoskeletal ultrasound computed tomography (USCT), but bone-related scattering, attenuation, and signal degradation make it highly sensitive to the accuracy of the initial acoustic-property distributions and prone to cycle skipping. First-arrival traveltimes provide important kinematic information for initial-model construction, yet conventional trace-wise picking is unreliable when arrivals are weak, spatially heterogeneous, or buried in system noise. We propose a learning-assisted reconstruction pipeline that combines segmentation-based first-arrival extraction with hybrid full-waveform inversion (HFWI), which incorporates Rytov-approximation-based traveltime information together with waveform fitting during the early inversion stage. A lightweight 2D U-Net treats the first-arrival trajectory across receiver channels as a first-break segmentation target and exploits its spatial continuity rather than processing each trace independently. To address both limited manual annotations and the simulation-to-real gap, the network is pretrained on task-specific simulations augmented with real system-noise recordings, followed by stage-wise training with progressively increased signal degradation and decoder-only fine-tuning using limited weakly labeled experimental data. The method is evaluated on in vitro phantom, ex vivo bovine-limb, and in vivo human-thigh datasets. Compared with conventional STA/LTA picking, the proposed network yields more spatially coherent first-arrival trajectories, lower mean extraction errors, and processes a full-matrix-capture dataset within seconds. When integrated into HFWI, the extracted arrivals improve initial-model construction and lead to stable subsequent FWI reconstructions, including challenging cases with estimated local first-arrival SNRs below 3 dB.

</details>


### [163] [Flow Matching-Based PET Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20112)
*Fumio Hashimoto,Ziqian Huang,Tatsuya Yokota,Kuang Gong*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于flow matching的PET图像重建方法，包括PET-FlowDPS（融合Poisson似然引导和EM预条件器）以及将预训练flow matching模型作为先验的模型化重建方法，在脑部PET数据集上取得了优于对照方法的偏差-方差权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽在PET重建中表现优异，但需要大量反向采样步骤并在采样过程中反复进行数据一致性更新，计算成本高。Flow matching能够直接从中间状态估计干净图像，使数据一致性精炼与流传播分离，提供更高效的替代方案。

Method: 1）提出PET-FlowDPS，将基于Poisson似然的引导和EM预条件器集成到FlowDPS框架中；2）提出模型化PET重建方法，将预训练的flow matching模型作为先验，在近似贝叶斯框架中统一解释基于流的先验、PET数据精炼和随机传播。

Result: 使用[18F]FDG脑部PET数据集进行实验，在不同剂量水平下，所提方法的偏差-方差权衡优于其他参考方法。

Conclusion: Flow matching作为一种生成式先验在定量PET图像重建中展现出潜力，提供了优于扩散模型的重建效率和重建质量。

Abstract: Generative models have shown strong potential for positron emission tomography (PET) image reconstruction. Although diffusion model-based reconstruction methods have demonstrated promising performance, they often require many reverse sampling steps with data-consistency updates incorporated into the sampling process. Flow matching offers an attractive alternative because it can directly estimate clean images from intermediate states, allowing data-consistency refinement to be separated from flow propagation. In this work, we proposed flow matching-based PET image reconstruction methods. We first established PET-FlowDPS by incorporating Poisson likelihood guidance with an expectation-maximization (EM)-based preconditioner into the FlowDPS framework. We then proposed a model-based PET reconstruction method that used a pretrained flow matching model as a prior, in which the flow-based prior, PET data refinement, and stochastic propagation were interpreted within an approximate Bayesian framework. Experimental results using [$^{\text{18}}\text{F}$]FDG brain PET datasets showed that the proposed method achieved better bias-variance trade-offs across different dose levels compared with other reference methods. These results demonstrated the potential of flow matching as a generative prior for quantitative PET image reconstruction.

</details>
